Method           CL_JVA_MIG_CORRECT_ACDOCA=====CM003       Level 1    Page 8
Object           CL_JVA_MIG_CORRECT_ACDOCA                 Lines 120
System           OCQ / 500   User SAP_ABAP   19.10.2026 14:49:37

1        method correct_acdoca_lines.
2
3          data: ls_jvso              type jvso,
4                ls_acdoca            type acdoca_jv,
5                lv_doc_stats_updated type abap_bool,
6                lv_docln_acd         type acdoca-docln,
7                lt_acd_docln         type hashed table of acdoca-docln
8                                     with unique key table_line.
9
10         loop at ct_log_doc assigning field-symbol(<ls_log>).
11
12           if <ls_log>-issues is initial or
13              <ls_log>-error = gc_error_type-error.
14             add_item_info_for_line( iv_update = abap_false
15                                     is_log    = <ls_log> ).
16             continue.
17           endif.
18
19           if ms_progpars-analysis_only = abap_true.
20             add_message_to_log(
21               exporting
22                 iv_msg_text   = space
23                 iv_msg_type   = gc_error_type-warning
24                 iv_is_upd_msg = abap_true
25               changing
26                 cs_log = <ls_log> ).
27             add_item_info_for_line( iv_update = abap_false
28                                     is_log    = <ls_log> ).
29             continue.
30           endif.
31
32           clear ls_jvso.
33           read table it_jvso_doc into ls_jvso
34                                   with key docnr = <ls_log>-docnr
35                                            docln = <ls_log>-docln
36                                   binary search.
37           if ls_jvso is initial.
38             add_message_to_log(
39               exporting
40                 iv_msg_text = conv string( 'Technical error: JVSO1 line not found in internal table IT_JVSO_DOC'(tej) )
41                 iv_msg_type = gc_error_type-error
42               changing
43                 cs_log = <ls_log> ).
44             add_item_info_for_line( iv_update = abap_false
45                                     is_log    = <ls_log> ).
46             continue.
47           endif.
48
49           "Get the ACDDOCA lines to which the JV doc line was assigned
50           read table is_line_assignments-jv_acd into data(ls_line_assignments)
51                                                 with key docnr = ls_jvso-docnr
52                                                          docln = ls_jvso-docln
53                                                 binary search.
54           if sy-subrc = 0.
55             data(lt_docln) = ls_line_assignments-t_acd_docln.
56           else.
57             clear lt_docln.
58             append <ls_log>-docln to lt_docln.
59           endif.
60
61           loop at lt_docln into data(lv_docln).
62
63             lv_docln_acd = |{ lv_docln alpha = in }|.
64
65             "As multiple assignments are possible, check if the ACDOCA line
66             "has already been processed before
67             read table lt_acd_docln transporting no fields
68                                     with key table_line = lv_docln_acd.
69             if sy-subrc = 0.
70               loop at ct_log_doc into data(ls_log_previous)
71                                  where docnr     = <ls_log>-docnr
72                                    and docln     < <ls_log>-docln
73                                    and docln_acd = <ls_log>-docln_acd.
74                 ls_log_previous-docln = <ls_log>-docln.
75                 add_item_info_for_line( iv_update = abap_true
76                                         is_log    = ls_log_previous ).
77                 update_line_statistics_updates( ls_log_previous ).
78                 exit.
79               endloop.
80               if sy-subrc <> 0.
81                 add_item_info_for_line( iv_update = abap_true
82                                         is_log    = <ls_log> ).
83                 update_line_statistics_updates( <ls_log> ).
84               endif.
85               continue.
86             else.
87               collect lv_docln_acd into lt_acd_docln.
88             endif.
89
90             clear ls_acdoca.
91             read table it_acdoca_doc into ls_acdoca
92                                      with key rldnr = <ls_log>-rldnr_acd
93                                               gjahr = <ls_log>-gjahr_acd
94                                               belnr = <ls_log>-belnr_acd
95                                               docln = lv_docln_acd.
96
97             correct_acdoca_line(
98               exporting
99                 is_jvso             = ls_jvso
100                is_acdoca           = ls_acdoca
101                iv_ledger           = iv_ledger
102                iv_is_reversal_line = ls_acdoca-rev_line
103              changing
104                cs_log              = <ls_log> ).
105
106            update_line_statistics_updates( <ls_log> ).
107
108            if lv_doc_stats_updated = abap_false.
109              lv_doc_stats_updated = abap_true.
110              update_doc_statistics_updates( <ls_log> ).
111            endif.
112
113            add_item_info_for_line( iv_update = abap_true
114                                    is_log    = <ls_log> ).
115
116          endloop.
117
118        endloop.
119
120      endmethod.
*--- End of CL_JVA_MIG_CORRECT_ACDOCA=====CM003 - 120 lines ---

1        method get_acdoca_line_for_jv.
2
3          data: ls_acdoca           type acdoca_jv,
4                lv_is_reversal_line type abap_bool,
5                ls_message          type bapiret2.
6
7          if is_jvso-refdocct = 'K'.
8            data(lv_is_co_line) = abap_true.
9          endif.
10
11         case cs_log-activ.
12           when gc_jvlinetype-equity_change.
13             if is_jvso-docln = '001'.
14               lv_is_reversal_line = abap_true.
15             endif.
16           when gc_jvlinetype-eq_adjust_reversal.
17             lv_is_reversal_line = abap_true.
18           when gc_jvlinetype-suspense.
19             lv_is_reversal_line = abap_true.
20         endcase.
21
22         rt_acdoca = get_acdoca_line_by_ref(
23                       exporting
24                         is_doc         = is_doc
25                         is_jvso        = is_jvso
26                         iv_tabix       = iv_tabix
27                         it_acdoca_doc  = it_acdoca_doc
28                         it_acdoca_all  = it_acdoca_all
29                         it_acdoca_int  = it_acdoca_int
30                         it_acdoca_mig  = it_acdoca_mig
31                         it_corr_log    = it_corr_log
32                         iv_is_rev_line = lv_is_reversal_line
33                       changing
34                         cs_log         = cs_log ).
35
36         if ms_progpars-search_by_accounting_data = abap_true and
37            lines( rt_acdoca ) = 0                            and
38            cs_log-jvdoctype <> gc_jvdoctype-fi_doc           and
39            cs_log-jvdoctype <> gc_jvdoctype-co_doc.
40
41           rt_acdoca = get_acdoca_line_by_acc_data(
42                         exporting
43                           is_doc              = is_doc
44                           is_jvso             = is_jvso
45                           it_acdoca_doc       = it_acdoca_doc
46                           iv_is_reversal_line = lv_is_reversal_line
47                           iv_wsl              = conv fins_vwcur12( is_jvso-tsl )
48                           iv_hsl              = conv fins_vwcur12( is_jvso-hsl )
49                         changing
50                           cs_log              = cs_log ).
51
52           if lines( rt_acdoca ) > 0.
53             add_message_to_log(
54               exporting
55                 iv_msg_text = 'ACDOCA line found by accounting data for JV doc line'(afa) && ` ` && condense( |{ is_jvso-docln alpha = out }| )
56                 iv_msg_type = gc_error_type-info
57               changing
58                 cs_log = cs_log ).
59           endif.
60
61         endif.
62
63         loop at rt_acdoca assigning field-symbol(<ls_acdoca>)
64                                     where ksl_gl = 0.
65           determine_jv_amnts_in_acd_line( changing cs_acdoca = <ls_acdoca> ).
66         endloop.
67
68         if lines( rt_acdoca ) > 1.
69           rt_acdoca = multiple_acd_line_processing(
70                         exporting
71                           is_doc              = is_doc
72                           is_jvso             = is_jvso
73                           it_acdoca_sel       = rt_acdoca
74                           it_acdoca_original  = rt_acdoca
75                           is_line_assignments = is_line_assignments
76                           iv_do_heuristic     = ms_progpars-heuristic_line_assignment
77                           iv_compress_by_pc   = iv_compress_by_pc
78                         importing
79                           et_acd_compr_info   = et_acd_compr_info
80                         changing
81                           cs_log              = cs_log ).
82         endif.
83
84         if lines( rt_acdoca ) <> 1.
85           if is_jvso-docln is not initial.
86             data(lv_docln_info) = condense( |{ is_jvso-docln alpha = out }| ).
87           else.
88             read table it_jv_compr_info assigning field-symbol(<ls_jv_compr_info>)
89                                         with key tabix_aggr = is_jvso-tabix_aggr.
90             if sy-subrc = 0 and <ls_jv_compr_info> is assigned.
91               data(lv_line_count) = lines( <ls_jv_compr_info>-t_docln_original ).
92               if lv_line_count > 10.
93                 lv_docln_info = `{` && lv_line_count && ` ` && 'lines'(lns) && `}`.
94               else.
95                 loop at <ls_jv_compr_info>-t_docln_original into data(ls_line_info).
96                   if lv_docln_info is not initial.
97                     lv_docln_info &&= `+`.
98                   endif.
99                   lv_docln_info &&= condense( |{ ls_line_info-docln alpha = out }| ).
100                endloop.
101              endif.
102            endif.
103            if <ls_jv_compr_info> is not assigned.
104              lv_docln_info = condense( |{ is_jvso-docln alpha = out }| ).
105            endif.
106          endif.
107
108          if is_jvso-reffidln is not initial.
109            data(lv_refln_info) = 'FI doc line'(fil)   && ` ` && condense( |{ is_jvso-reffidln alpha = out }| ).
110          endif.
111          if is_jvso-refdocln is not initial and
112             is_jvso-refdocln <> is_jvso-reffidln.
113            if lv_refln_info is not initial.
114              lv_refln_info &&= `, `.
115            endif.
116            lv_refln_info &&= 'Ref. doc line'(rdl) && ` ` && condense( |{ is_jvso-refdocln alpha = out }| ).
117          endif.
118          if lv_refln_info is not initial.
119            lv_docln_info &&= ` [` && lv_refln_info && `]`.
120          endif.
121        endif.
122
123        if lines( rt_acdoca ) = 0 and
124           ( is_jvso-tsl    <> 0 or
125             is_jvso-hsl    <> 0 or
126             is_jvso-ksl    <> 0 or
127             is_jvso-hsl_4c <> 0 or
128             is_jvso-ksl_4c <> 0 ).
129
130          if is_jvso-activ    = gc_jvlinetype-balancing_line or
131             is_jvso-refactiv = gc_jvlinetype-balancing_line.
132            data(lv_issue_type) = gc_issue_type-balancing_line_issue.
133          else.
134            lv_issue_type = gc_issue_type-line_missing.
135          endif.
136
137          cs_log-issues = lv_issue_type.
138
139          cs_log-corr_type = determine_correction_type( iv_corr_type  = space
140                                                        iv_issue_type = lv_issue_type
141                                                        iv_is_co_line = lv_is_co_line ).
142
143          if cs_log-corr_type = gc_corr_type-correction_not_possible.
144            data(lv_error_type) = gc_error_type-error.
145          else.
146            lv_error_type = gc_error_type-warning.
147          endif.
148
149          add_message_to_log(
150            exporting
151              iv_msg_text = 'ACDOCA line not found for JV doc line'(anf) && ` ` && lv_docln_info
152              iv_msg_type = lv_error_type
153            changing
154              cs_log = cs_log ).
155
156        elseif lines( rt_acdoca ) = 1.
157
158          read table rt_acdoca index 1 into ls_acdoca.      "#EC CI_NOORDER
159          if sy-subrc = 0.
160            cs_log-rldnr_acd = ls_acdoca-rldnr.
161            cs_log-belnr_acd = ls_acdoca-belnr.
162            cs_log-docln_acd = ls_acdoca-docln.
163            cs_log-gjahr_acd = ls_acdoca-gjahr.
164          endif.
165
166        elseif lines( rt_acdoca ) > 1.
167
168          if cs_log-heuristic_hit = abap_true.
169            add_message_to_log(
170              exporting
171                iv_msg_text = 'Multiple ACDOCA lines assigned to JV doc line'(mlh) && ` `  && lv_docln_info
172                iv_msg_type = gc_error_type-info
173              changing
174                cs_log = cs_log ).
175            read table rt_acdoca index 1 into ls_acdoca.    "#EC CI_NOORDER
176            if sy-subrc = 0.
177              cs_log-rldnr_acd = ls_acdoca-rldnr.
178              cs_log-belnr_acd = ls_acdoca-belnr.
179              cs_log-docln_acd = ls_acdoca-docln.
180              cs_log-gjahr_acd = ls_acdoca-gjahr.
181            endif.
182          else.
183            add_message_to_log(
184              exporting
185                iv_msg_text = 'Multiple ACDOCA lines found for JV doc line'(mlf) && ` ` && lv_docln_info
186                iv_msg_type = gc_error_type-error
187              changing
188                cs_log = cs_log ).
189            clear rt_acdoca.
190          endif.
191
192        endif.
193
194      endmethod.

339                                          and ksl_4c = is_jvso-ksl_4c
340                                          and buzei  is initial.
341                    append ls_acdoca to rt_acdoca.
342                  endloop.
343                endif.
344              endif.
345
346            endif.
347
348          endif.
349
350          if rt_acdoca[] is initial and
351             is_doc-is_fi_doc = abap_false.
352
353            read table it_acdoca_int transporting no fields
354                                     with key rbukrs      = is_jvso-rbukrs
355                                              gjahr       = is_jvso-ryear
356                                              prec_awtyp  = is_jvso-awtyp
357                                              prec_aworg  = is_jvso-aworg
358                                              prec_awref  = is_jvso-refdocnr
359                                              prec_awitem = lv_awitem
360                                     binary search.
361            loop at it_acdoca_int into ls_acdoca
362                                  from sy-tabix.
363              if ls_acdoca-rbukrs      <> is_jvso-rbukrs   or
364                 ls_acdoca-gjahr       <> is_jvso-ryear    or

368                 ls_acdoca-prec_awitem <> lv_awitem.
369                exit.
370              endif.
371              append ls_acdoca to rt_acdoca.
372            endloop.

374            if rt_acdoca[] is initial.
375              "In some cases the PREC_AWORG and PREC_AWTYP values can be initial (cause unknown)
376              read table it_acdoca_int transporting no fields
377                                       with key rbukrs      = is_jvso-rbukrs
378                                                gjahr       = is_jvso-ryear
379                                                prec_awtyp  = space
380                                                prec_aworg  = space
381                                                prec_awref  = is_jvso-refdocnr
382                                                prec_awitem = lv_awitem

385                                    from sy-tabix.

387                   ls_acdoca-gjahr       <> is_jvso-ryear    or
388                   ls_acdoca-prec_awtyp  <> space            or
389                   ls_acdoca-prec_aworg  <> space            or

391                   ls_acdoca-prec_awitem <> lv_awitem.
392                  exit.
393                endif.
394                append ls_acdoca to rt_acdoca.
395              endloop.
396            endif.
397
398          endif.
399
400          if rt_acdoca[] is initial.
401
402            "Check if the document was already treated by checking for the
403            "entry in the log table
404            ls_corr_log = get_log_item( is_doc      = is_doc
405                                        it_corr_log = it_corr_log ).
406            if ls_corr_log is not initial.
407              read table it_acdoca_all transporting no fields
408                                       with key rbukrs = is_jvso-rbukrs
409                                                gjahr  = ls_corr_log-gjahr_new
410                                                belnr  = ls_corr_log-belnr_new

413                                    from sy-tabix.

415                   ls_acdoca-gjahr  <> ls_corr_log-gjahr_new or

417                  exit.
418                endif.

424                endif.
425              endloop.
426            endif.
427          endif.
428
429        endif.

431        if rt_acdoca[] is initial and
432           it_acdoca_mig[] is not initial.

434          "Check if the document was posted by S4HANA migration functionality
435

437                                   with key rbukrs = is_jvso-rbukrs
438                                            gjahr  = is_jvso-ryear
439                                            awref  = is_jvso-refdocnr

445                                  from sy-tabix.

462                endif.

466                  append ls_acdoca to rt_acdoca.
467                endif.
468              endif.

471
472          endif.
473
474        endif.
475
476        if rt_acdoca[] is not initial  or
477           lv_search_amnt = abap_false.
478          return.
479        endif.
480
481        "If the JV lines contines both positive and negative amounts,
482        "the search needs to be executed separately for each case
483        if is_jvso-tsl    > 0. add 1 to lv_pos_count. endif.
484        if is_jvso-hsl    > 0. add 1 to lv_pos_count. endif.
485        if is_jvso-ksl    > 0. add 1 to lv_pos_count. endif.
486        if is_jvso-hsl_4c > 0. add 1 to lv_pos_count. endif.
487        if is_jvso-ksl_4c > 0. add 1 to lv_pos_count. endif.
488
489        if is_jvso-tsl    < 0. add 1 to lv_neg_count. endif.
490        if is_jvso-hsl    < 0. add 1 to lv_neg_count. endif.
491        if is_jvso-ksl    < 0. add 1 to lv_neg_count. endif.
492        if is_jvso-hsl_4c < 0. add 1 to lv_neg_count. endif.
493        if is_jvso-ksl_4c < 0. add 1 to lv_neg_count. endif.
494
495        if lv_pos_count > 0 and lv_neg_count > 0.
496
497          data(ls_jvso_pos) = is_jvso.
498          if ls_jvso_pos-tsl    < 0. clear ls_jvso_pos-tsl.    endif.
499          if ls_jvso_pos-hsl    < 0. clear ls_jvso_pos-hsl.    endif.
500          if ls_jvso_pos-ksl    < 0. clear ls_jvso_pos-ksl.    endif.
501          if ls_jvso_pos-hsl_4c < 0. clear ls_jvso_pos-hsl_4c. endif.
502          if ls_jvso_pos-ksl_4c < 0. clear ls_jvso_pos-ksl_4c. endif.
503          data(ls_jvso_neg) = is_jvso.
504          if ls_jvso_neg-tsl    > 0. clear ls_jvso_neg-tsl.    endif.
505          if ls_jvso_neg-hsl    > 0. clear ls_jvso_neg-hsl.    endif.
506          if ls_jvso_neg-ksl    > 0. clear ls_jvso_neg-ksl.    endif.

508          if ls_jvso_neg-ksl_4c > 0. clear ls_jvso_neg-ksl_4c. endif.

511                                  exporting
512                                    is_doc         = is_doc
513                                    is_jvso        = ls_jvso_pos
514                                    iv_tabix       = iv_tabix
515                                    it_acdoca_doc  = it_acdoca_doc
516                                    it_acdoca_all  = it_acdoca_all

521                                  changing
522                                    cs_log         = cs_log ).

525                                  exporting
526                                    is_doc         = is_doc
527                                    is_jvso        = ls_jvso_neg

533                                    it_corr_log    = it_corr_log
534                                    iv_is_rev_line = iv_is_rev_line

1        method determine_correction_type.
2
3          data: lv_corr_type type char1.
4
5          case iv_issue_type.
6

21                gc_issue_type-wrong_amount_sign.

1        method add_message_to_log.
2
3          data: ls_message      type bapiret2,
4                lv_message_type like iv_msg_type,
5                lv_message_id   type string,
6                lv_message_text type string.
7
8          if is_message is not initial.
9
10           ls_message = is_message.
11           lv_message_type = ls_message-type.
12
13           if ls_message-id     <> 'GJ' and
14              ls_message-number <> '000'.
15             lv_message_id = ls_message-id && `-` && ls_message-number.
16           endif.
17
18           if ls_message-message is not initial.
19             if lv_message_id is initial.
20               lv_message_text = ls_message-message.
21             else.
22               lv_message_text = lv_message_id && `: ` && ls_message-message.
23             endif.
24           elseif ls_message-id     is not initial and
25                  ls_message-type   is not initial and
26                  ls_message-number is not initial.
27             message id ls_message-id type ls_message-type number ls_message-number
28                     with ls_message-message_v1 ls_message-message_v2
29                          ls_message-message_v3 ls_message-message_v4
30                     into lv_message_text.
31             lv_message_text = lv_message_id && `: ` && lv_message_text.
32           else.
33             lv_message_text = lv_message_id.
34           endif.
35
36           if cs_log-comment is not initial.
37             cs_log-comment &&= ` - `.
38           endif.
39           cs_log-comment &&= lv_message_text.
40
41         else.
42
43           lv_message_text = iv_msg_text.
44           lv_message_type = iv_msg_type.
45
46           if iv_is_upd_msg = abap_true.
47             if cs_log-comment is initial.
48               loop at cs_log-messages into ls_message
49                                       where message is not initial.
50                 if ls_message-message(4) = `-`.
51                   continue.
52                 endif.
53                 if ls_message-message(2) = `-`.
54                   ls_message-message = ls_message-message+2.
55                 endif.
56                 if cs_log-comment is initial.
57                   cs_log-comment = ls_message-message.
58                 else.
59                   cs_log-comment &&= ` ` && ls_message-message.
60                 endif.
61               endloop.
62             endif.
63             if lv_message_text is not initial.
64               if cs_log-comment is not initial.
65                 cs_log-comment = lv_message_text && ` - ` && cs_log-comment.
66               else.
67                 cs_log-comment = lv_message_text.
68               endif.
69             endif.
70           else.
71             if cs_log-comment is not initial.
72               cs_log-comment &&= ` - `.
73             endif.
74             cs_log-comment &&= lv_message_text.
75           endif.
76
77         endif.
78
79         if lv_message_text is not initial                      and
80            ms_progpars-do_not_store_issue_details = abap_false and
81            sy-batch is initial.
82           case iv_indent_level.
83             when 1.
84               lv_message_text = `- ` && lv_message_text.
85             when 2.
86               lv_message_text = `-   ` && lv_message_text.
87           endcase.
88
89           if ls_message-type is initial and
90              lv_message_type is not initial.
91             ls_message-type = lv_message_type.
92           endif.
93           ls_message-message = lv_message_text.
94
95           if iv_position > 0.
96             insert ls_message into cs_log-messages index iv_position.
97           else.
98             append ls_message to cs_log-messages.
99           endif.
100
101        endif.
102
103        cs_log-error = set_error_type( iv_old_error = cs_log-error
104                                       iv_new_error = lv_message_type ).

106      endmethod.

1        method set_error_type.
2
3          rv_error = iv_old_error.
4
5          if iv_new_error is initial.
6            return.
7          endif.
8
9          if rv_error is initial.
10           rv_error = iv_new_error.
11           return.
12         endif.
13
14         case iv_new_error.
15           when gc_error_type-shortdump.
16             rv_error = iv_new_error.
17           when gc_error_type-abend.
18             if iv_old_error <> gc_error_type-shortdump.
19               rv_error = iv_new_error.
20             endif.
21           when gc_error_type-error.
22             if iv_old_error <> gc_error_type-shortdump and
23                iv_old_error <> gc_error_type-abend.
24               rv_error = iv_new_error.
25             endif.
26           when gc_error_type-warning.
27             if iv_old_error <> gc_error_type-shortdump and
28                iv_old_error <> gc_error_type-abend     and
29                iv_old_error <> gc_error_type-error.
30               rv_error = iv_new_error.
31             endif.
32           when gc_error_type-statement.
33             if iv_old_error <> gc_error_type-shortdump and
34                iv_old_error <> gc_error_type-abend     and
35                iv_old_error <> gc_error_type-error     and
36                iv_old_error <> gc_error_type-warning.
37               rv_error = iv_new_error.
38             endif.
39           when gc_error_type-info.
40             if iv_old_error <> gc_error_type-shortdump and
41                iv_old_error <> gc_error_type-abend     and
42                iv_old_error <> gc_error_type-error     and
43                iv_old_error <> gc_error_type-warning   and
44                iv_old_error <> gc_error_type-statement.
45               rv_error = iv_new_error.
46             endif.
47           when others.
48             "Do nothing
49         endcase.
50
51       endmethod.

1        method compress_jv_doc.
2
3          data: lt_jvso_aggr     type jvso_t,
4                ls_jv_compr_info type gty_s_jv_compr_info,
5                lv_tabix_hit     type sy-tabix,
6                ls_line_info     type gty_s_jv_line_info.
7
8          clear et_jv_compr_info.
9          et_jvso[] = it_jvso_original[].
10
11         "Check if the document has different ORECID values, tax IDs, cost objects
12         "or profit centers per FI doc line
13         sort et_jvso by reffidoc reffidln rjvnam regrou rpartn rrecin bilid.
14
15         loop at et_jvso into data(ls_jvso).
16
17           ls_line_info-docnr = ls_jvso-docnr.
18           ls_line_info-docln = ls_jvso-docln.
19
20           clear: ls_jvso-docnr,
21                  ls_jvso-docln,
22                  ls_jvso-drcrk,
23                  ls_jvso-rbusa,   "RBUSA is not updated reliably in JVSO1
24                  ls_jvso-tabix_aggr,
25                  ls_jvso-cpudt,
26                  ls_jvso-cputm,
27                  ls_jvso-sgtxt.
28
29           if ls_jvso-orecid is not initial.
30             data(lv_orecid_omitted) = abap_true.
31           endif.
32           clear: ls_jvso-orecid.
33
34           if ls_jvso-mwskz is not initial.
35             data(lv_tax_id_omitted) = abap_true.
36           endif.
37           clear: ls_jvso-mwskz.
38
39           if ls_jvso-ebeln is not initial or
40              ls_jvso-ebelp is not initial.
41             data(lv_po_omitted) = abap_true.
42           endif.
43           clear: ls_jvso-ebeln,
44                  ls_jvso-ebelp.
45
46           if iv_compress_by_co = abap_true.
47             if ls_jvso-rcntr  is not initial or
48                ls_jvso-rordnr is not initial or
49                ls_jvso-rprojk is not initial or
50                ls_jvso-nplnr  is not initial or
51                ls_jvso-aufpl  is not initial or
52                ls_jvso-aplzl  is not initial or
53                ls_jvso-vornr  is not initial.
54               data(lv_co_obj_omitted) = abap_true.
55             endif.
56             clear: ls_jvso-rcntr,
57                    ls_jvso-rordnr,
58                    ls_jvso-rprojk,
59                    ls_jvso-nplnr,
60                    ls_jvso-aufpl,
61                    ls_jvso-aplzl,
62                    ls_jvso-vornr.
63           endif.
64
65           if iv_compress_by_pc = abap_true.
66             if ls_jvso-rprctr is not initial.
67               data(lv_pc_omitted) = abap_true.
68             endif.
69             clear: ls_jvso-rprctr.
70           endif.
71
72           collect ls_jvso into lt_jvso_aggr.
73           data(lv_tabix_aggr) = sy-tabix.
74
75           "Save links between the aggregated item and the original items
76           "First check if entry already exist
77           read table et_jv_compr_info assigning field-symbol(<ls_jv_compr_info>)
78                                       with key tabix_aggr = lv_tabix_aggr.
79           if sy-subrc = 0 and
80              <ls_jv_compr_info> is assigned.
81             <ls_jv_compr_info>-count += 1.
82             append ls_line_info to <ls_jv_compr_info>-t_docln_original.
83           else.
84             clear ls_jv_compr_info.
85             ls_jv_compr_info-tabix_aggr = lv_tabix_aggr.
86             ls_jv_compr_info-count = 1.
87             append ls_line_info to ls_jv_compr_info-t_docln_original.
88             ls_jv_compr_info-reffidoc = ls_jvso-reffidoc.
89             ls_jv_compr_info-reffidln = ls_jvso-reffidln.
90             if lv_orecid_omitted = abap_true.
91               ls_jv_compr_info-orecid_omitted = abap_true.
92             endif.
93             if lv_tax_id_omitted = abap_true.
94               ls_jv_compr_info-tax_id_omitted = abap_true.
95             endif.
96             if lv_po_omitted = abap_true.
97               ls_jv_compr_info-po_omitted = abap_true.
98             endif.
99             if lv_co_obj_omitted = abap_true.
100              ls_jv_compr_info-co_obj_omitted = abap_true.
101            endif.
102            if lv_pc_omitted = abap_true.
103              ls_jv_compr_info-pc_omitted = abap_true.
104            endif.
105            append ls_jv_compr_info to et_jv_compr_info.
106          endif.
107
108        endloop.
109
110        sort et_jvso by docnr docln.
111
112        loop at et_jv_compr_info into ls_jv_compr_info
113                                 where count > 1.
114          rv_compressed = abap_true.
115          read table lt_jvso_aggr into data(ls_jvso_aggr)
116                                  index ls_jv_compr_info-tabix_aggr.
117          if sy-subrc = 0.
118            loop at ls_jv_compr_info-t_docln_original into ls_line_info.
119              delete et_jvso where docnr = ls_line_info-docnr
120                               and docln = ls_line_info-docln.
121            endloop.

1        method add_compression_message.
2
3          data: lv_omitted_objects type string,
4                lv_tabix_aggr      type sy-tabix,
5                lv_line_string     type string,
6                lv_message_text    type string.
7
8          clear ev_all_amnts_zero.
9
10         lv_tabix_aggr = is_jvso-tabix_aggr.
11
12         read table ct_jv_compr_info
13                    assigning field-symbol(<ls_jv_compr_info>)
14                    with key tabix_aggr = lv_tabix_aggr.
15
16         if sy-subrc <> 0 or
17            <ls_jv_compr_info> is not assigned.
18           return.
19         endif.
20
21         if <ls_jv_compr_info>-message_added = abap_true.
22           return.
23         endif.
24
25         if <ls_jv_compr_info>-co_obj_omitted = abap_true.
26           lv_omitted_objects = 'cost objects'(cob).
27         endif.
28         if <ls_jv_compr_info>-pc_omitted = abap_true.
29           if lv_omitted_objects is not initial.
30             lv_omitted_objects &&= `, `.
31           endif.
32           lv_omitted_objects = 'profit center'(prc).
33         endif.
34         if <ls_jv_compr_info>-orecid_omitted = abap_true.
35           if lv_omitted_objects is not initial.
36             lv_omitted_objects &&= `, `.
37           endif.
38           lv_omitted_objects = 'orig. RI'(ori).
39         endif.
40         if <ls_jv_compr_info>-tax_id_omitted = abap_true.
41           if lv_omitted_objects is not initial.
42             lv_omitted_objects &&= `, `.
43           endif.
44           lv_omitted_objects = 'tax ID'(tid).
45         endif.
46         if <ls_jv_compr_info>-po_omitted = abap_true.
47           if lv_omitted_objects is not initial.
48             lv_omitted_objects &&= `, `.
49           endif.
50           lv_omitted_objects = 'Purch. Order'(puo).
51         endif.
52         if lv_omitted_objects is initial.
53           lv_omitted_objects = 'technical fields'(tcf).
54         endif.
55
56         <ls_jv_compr_info>-message_added = abap_true.
57
58         data(lv_line_count) = lines( <ls_jv_compr_info>-t_docln_original ).
59         if lv_line_count > 20.
60           lv_line_string = `{` && lv_line_count && ` ` && 'lines'(lns) && `}`.
61         else.
62           loop at <ls_jv_compr_info>-t_docln_original into data(ls_line_info).
63             if lv_line_string is not initial.
64               lv_line_string &&= `, `.
65             endif.
66             lv_line_string &&= condense( |{ ls_line_info-docln alpha = out }| ).
67           endloop.
68         endif.
69
70         lv_message_text = 'JV doc was compressed by these lines:'(jdc) && ` ` && lv_line_string.
71
72         if is_jvso-tsl    = 0 and
73            is_jvso-hsl    = 0 and
74            is_jvso-ksl    = 0 and
75            is_jvso-hsl_4c = 0 and
76            is_jvso-ksl_4c = 0.
77           ev_all_amnts_zero = abap_true.
78           lv_message_text &&= ` [` && 'All amounts sum up to zero'(aaz) && `]`.
79         endif.
80
81         add_message_to_log(
82           exporting
83             iv_msg_text = lv_message_text
84             iv_msg_type = gc_error_type-info
85           changing
86             cs_log = cs_log ).
87
88       endmethod.

401             iv_recursion_level >= 4 ).
402          return.
403        endif.
404
405        "Try to aggregate the ACDOCA lines
406
407        loop at it_acdoca_sel assigning field-symbol(<ls_acdoca>).
408
409          data(lv_tabix_sel) = sy-tabix.
410          data(lv_docln) = <ls_acdoca>-docln.
411
412          "Check if the line was already assigned to a JV doc line
413          "If yes, it should take part in the compression
414          read table is_line_assignments-acd_jv transporting no fields
415                                                with key docln = lv_docln
416                                                binary search.
417          if sy-subrc = 0.
418            continue.
419          endif.
420
421          ls_acdoca = <ls_acdoca>.
422
423          if iv_recursion_level >= 0.
424            clear: ls_acdoca-docln,
425                   ls_acdoca-timestamp,
426                   ls_acdoca-sgtxt.
427          endif.
428
429          if iv_recursion_level >= 1.
430            clear: ls_acdoca-vname,
431                   ls_acdoca-egrup,
432                   ls_acdoca-recid,
433                   ls_acdoca-btype,
434                   ls_acdoca-vptnr,
435                   ls_acdoca-prodper.
436          endif.
437
438          if iv_recursion_level >= 2.
439            clear: ls_acdoca-co_belnr,
440                   ls_acdoca-awitem,
441                   ls_acdoca-co_buzei.
442            if ls_acdoca-accasty <> 'KS' and
443               ls_acdoca-accasty <> 'OR' and
444               ls_acdoca-accasty <> 'OV' and
445               ls_acdoca-accasty <> 'PR' and
446               ls_acdoca-accasty <> 'NV' and
447               ls_acdoca-accasty <> 'NP'.
448              clear: ls_acdoca-accasty,
449                     ls_acdoca-rcntr,
450                     ls_acdoca-aufnr,
451                     ls_acdoca-ps_psp_pnr,
452                     ls_acdoca-nplnr,
453                     ls_acdoca-nplnr_vorgn,
454                     ls_acdoca-vornr,
455                     ls_acdoca-runit.
456            endif.
457
458            if iv_recursion_level >= 3 and
459               iv_compress_by_pc  = abap_true.
460              clear: ls_acdoca-prctr.
461            endif.
462
463          endif.
464
465          collect ls_acdoca into lt_acdoca_aggr.
466          data(lv_tabix_aggr) = sy-tabix.
467
468          clear lt_docln.
469          if lv_docln is not initial.
470            append lv_docln to lt_docln.
471          else.
472            read table it_acd_compr_info assigning field-symbol(<ls_acd_compr_info>)
473                                         with key tabix_aggr = lv_tabix_sel.
474            if sy-subrc = 0 and
475               <ls_acd_compr_info> is assigned.
476              lt_docln[] = <ls_acd_compr_info>-t_docln_original[].
477            endif.
478          endif.
479
480          "Save links between the aggregated item and the original items
481          "First check if entry already exist
482          read table et_acd_compr_info assigning <ls_acd_compr_info>
483                                       with key tabix_aggr = lv_tabix_aggr.
484          if sy-subrc = 0 and
485             <ls_acd_compr_info> is assigned.
486            append lines of lt_docln to <ls_acd_compr_info>-t_docln_original.
487          else.
488            clear ls_acd_compr_info.
489            ls_acd_compr_info-tabix_aggr = lv_tabix_aggr.
490            ls_acd_compr_info-t_docln_original[] = lt_docln[].
491            append ls_acd_compr_info to et_acd_compr_info.
492          endif.
493
494        endloop.
495
496        rt_acdoca = multiple_acd_line_processing(
497                      exporting
498                        is_doc              = is_doc
499                        is_jvso             = is_jvso
500                        it_acdoca_sel       = lt_acdoca_aggr
501                        it_acdoca_original  = it_acdoca_original
502                        is_line_assignments = is_line_assignments
503                        iv_do_heuristic     = iv_do_heuristic
504                        iv_compress_by_pc   = iv_compress_by_pc
505                        iv_recursion_level  = iv_recursion_level + 1
506                        it_acd_compr_info   = et_acd_compr_info
507                      importing
508                        et_acd_compr_info   = et_acd_compr_info
509                      changing
510                        cs_log              = cs_log ).
511
512      endmethod.

1        method get_acdoca_doc_lines.
2
3          clear et_acdoca.
4
5          if cs_doc-is_fi_doc = abap_true.
6
7            read table it_acdoca_all transporting no fields
8                                     with key rldnr  = cs_doc-ledger
9                                              rbukrs = cs_doc-rbukrs
10                                             gjahr  = cs_doc-ryear
11                                             belnr  = cs_doc-reffidoc.
12           if sy-subrc = 0.
13             loop at it_acdoca_all into data(ls_acdoca)
14                                   from sy-tabix.
15               if ls_acdoca-rldnr  <> cs_doc-ledger or
16                  ls_acdoca-rbukrs <> cs_doc-rbukrs or
17                  ls_acdoca-gjahr  <> cs_doc-ryear  or
18                  ls_acdoca-belnr  <> cs_doc-reffidoc.
19                 exit.
20               endif.
21               append ls_acdoca to et_acdoca.
22
23               if ls_acdoca-mig_source is not initial.
24                 "If the document was migrated, determine if it was fully migrated
25                 "with a single migration source
26                 if cs_doc-mig_source is initial.
27                   cs_doc-mig_source = ls_acdoca-mig_source.
28                 elseif cs_doc-mig_source <> ls_acdoca-mig_source.
29                   clear cs_doc-mig_source.
30                 endif.
31               endif.
32
33             endloop.
34           endif.
35
36           if cs_doc-bstat is not initial or
37              cs_doc-mig_source = 'J'.
38             sort et_acdoca by rbukrs gjahr belnr docln.
39           else.
40             sort et_acdoca by rbukrs gjahr belnr buzei awitem.
41           endif.
42
43         elseif cs_doc-is_co_doc = abap_true.
44
45           read table it_acdoca_all transporting no fields
46                                    with key rldnr    = cs_doc-ledger
47                                             rbukrs   = cs_doc-rbukrs
48                                             gjahr    = cs_doc-ryear
49                                             co_belnr = cs_doc-refdocnr.
50           if sy-subrc = 0.
51             loop at it_acdoca_all into ls_acdoca
52                                   from sy-tabix.
53               if ls_acdoca-rldnr    <> cs_doc-ledger or
54                  ls_acdoca-rbukrs   <> cs_doc-rbukrs or
55                  ls_acdoca-gjahr    <> cs_doc-ryear  or
56                  ls_acdoca-co_belnr <> cs_doc-refdocnr.
57                 exit.
58               endif.
59               append ls_acdoca to et_acdoca.
60             endloop.
61           endif.
62
63           sort et_acdoca by rbukrs gjahr co_belnr co_buzei awitem.
64
65         elseif cs_doc-jvdoctype = gc_jvdoctype-cutback  or
66                cs_doc-jvdoctype = gc_jvdoctype-cash_call.
67
68           read table it_acdoca_all transporting no fields
69                                    with key rldnr  = cs_doc-ledger
70                                             rbukrs = cs_doc-rbukrs
71                                             gjahr  = cs_doc-ryear
72                                             zuonr  = cs_doc-zuonr.
73           if sy-subrc = 0.
74             loop at it_acdoca_all into ls_acdoca
75                                   from sy-tabix.
76               if ls_acdoca-rldnr  <> cs_doc-ledger or
77                  ls_acdoca-rbukrs <> cs_doc-rbukrs or
78                  ls_acdoca-gjahr  <> cs_doc-ryear  or
79                  ls_acdoca-zuonr  <> cs_doc-zuonr.
80                 exit.
81               endif.
82               append ls_acdoca to et_acdoca.
83             endloop.
84           endif.
85
86           if et_acdoca[] is initial.
87             data(ls_corr_log) = get_log_item( is_doc      = cs_doc
88                                               it_corr_log = it_corr_log ).
89             if ls_corr_log is not initial.
90               read table it_acdoca_all transporting no fields
91                                        with key rldnr  = cs_doc-ledger
92                                                 rbukrs = cs_doc-rbukrs
93                                                 gjahr  = ls_corr_log-gjahr_new
94                                                 belnr  = ls_corr_log-belnr_new
95                                        binary search.
96               loop at it_acdoca_all into ls_acdoca
97                                     from sy-tabix.
98                 if ls_acdoca-rldnr  <> cs_doc-ledger         or
99                    ls_acdoca-rbukrs <> cs_doc-rbukrs         or
100                   ls_acdoca-gjahr  <> ls_corr_log-gjahr_new or
101                   ls_acdoca-belnr  <> ls_corr_log-belnr_new.
102                  exit.
103                endif.
104                append ls_acdoca to et_acdoca.
105              endloop.
106            endif.
107          endif.
108
109          sort et_acdoca by rbukrs gjahr belnr buzei awitem.
110
111        endif.
112
113        if et_acdoca[] is initial and
114           mv_has_migrated_jv_docs = abap_true.
115
116          read table it_acdoca_mig transporting no fields
117                                   with key rldnr  = cs_doc-ledger
118                                            rbukrs = cs_doc-rbukrs
119                                            gjahr  = cs_doc-ryear
120                                            awref  = cs_doc-refdocnr
121                                   binary search.
122          loop at it_acdoca_mig into ls_acdoca
123                                from sy-tabix.
124            if ls_acdoca-rldnr  <> cs_doc-ledger or
125               ls_acdoca-rbukrs <> cs_doc-rbukrs or
126               ls_acdoca-gjahr  <> cs_doc-ryear  or
127               ls_acdoca-awref  <> cs_doc-refdocnr.
128              exit.
129            endif.
130            append ls_acdoca to et_acdoca.
131          endloop.
132
133          if cs_doc-bstat is not initial or
134             cs_doc-mig_source = 'J'.
135            sort et_acdoca by rbukrs gjahr belnr docln.
136          else.
137            sort et_acdoca by rbukrs gjahr belnr buzei awitem.
138          endif.
139
140        endif.
141
142      endmethod.

1        method make_where_clause.
2
3          data: lv_where_clause type char100,
4                lv_text         type char100,
5                lv_sus_year     type gjahr,
6                lv_sus_period   type poper,

8

11         lv_where_clause =     |rldnr = '{ iv_rldnr }'|  ##NO_TEXT.

23           append lv_where_clause to et_where_clause.

25           append lv_where_clause to et_where_clause.
26           lv_where_clause = `  )`  ##NO_TEXT.
27           append lv_where_clause to et_where_clause.

138        append lv_where_clause to et_where_clause.
139

141        "append lv_where_clause to et_where_clause.
142        "lv_where_clause = |AND msl   = '{ is_jvso-msl }'|  ##NO_TEXT.
143        "append lv_where_clause to et_where_clause.
144        "lv_where_clause = |AND mwskz = '{ is_jvso-mwskz }'|  ##NO_TEXT.

152        append lv_where_clause to et_where_clause.

154        append lv_where_clause to et_where_clause.

156        if iv_is_co_doc = abap_true.
157          lv_where_clause = |AND co_buzei = '{ is_jvso-refdocln+2(3) }'|  ##NO_TEXT.
158          append lv_where_clause to et_where_clause.
159        else.
160          lv_where_clause = `AND co_buzei = ''`  ##NO_TEXT.
161          append lv_where_clause to et_where_clause.
162        endif.
163
164        lv_where_clause = |AND wsl   = '{ is_jvso-tsl }'|  ##NO_TEXT.
165        append lv_where_clause to et_where_clause.
166        lv_where_clause = |AND hsl   = '{ is_jvso-hsl }'|  ##NO_TEXT.
167        append lv_where_clause to et_where_clause.
168
169        "Special handling for text field
170        if iv_search_with_sgtxt = abap_true and
171           not is_jvso-sgtxt is initial.
172          lv_text = is_jvso-sgtxt.
173          replace all occurrences of `'` in lv_text with `''`  ##NO_TEXT.
174          lv_where_clause = |AND sgtxt = '{ lv_text }'|  ##NO_TEXT.
175          append lv_where_clause to et_where_clause.
176        endif.
177
178      endmethod.

Object           CL_JVA_MIG_CORRECT_ACDOCA                 Lines 74
System           OCQ / 500   User SAP_ABAP   19.10.2026 14:49:37
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
1        method prepare_acdoca_document.
2
3          loop at ct_acdoca assigning field-symbol(<ls_acdoca>).
4
5            data(lv_tabix) = sy-tabix.
6
7            "Special handling for migrated documents: Delete irrelevant items
8            if mv_has_migrated_jv_docs = abap_true.
9              if <ls_acdoca>-mig_source is not initial and
10                <ls_acdoca>-mig_source <> 'J'         and
11                <ls_acdoca>-awtyp      <> 'JVAME'     and
12                <ls_acdoca>-awtyp      <> 'JVAM'      and
13                <ls_acdoca>-vname      is initial.
14               delete ct_acdoca index lv_tabix.
15               continue.
16             endif.
17           elseif mv_has_migrated_docs = abap_true.
18             if cs_doc-jvdoctype = gc_jvdoctype-fi_doc.
19               if <ls_acdoca>-mig_source is not initial and
20                  <ls_acdoca>-mig_source <> 'G'         and
21                  <ls_acdoca>-vname      is initial.
22                 delete ct_acdoca index lv_tabix.
23                 continue.
24               endif.
25             endif.
26           endif.
27
28           if ms_progpars-exclude_balancing_lines = abap_true.
29             "Delete the venture balancing lines for FI docs
30             "and if it is a proper FI doc (BSTAT is initial)
31             if cs_doc-jvdoctype = gc_jvdoctype-fi_doc   or
32                cs_doc-jvdoctype = gc_jvdoctype-cutback  or
33                cs_doc-jvdoctype = gc_jvdoctype-cash_call.
34               if cs_doc-bstat is initial.
35                 if cs_doc-mig_source = 'J'.
36                   if <ls_acdoca>-awitem is initial.
37                     delete ct_acdoca index lv_tabix.
38                     cs_doc-gl_no_of_lines_excluded += 1.
39                     continue.
40                   endif.
41                 else.
42                   if <ls_acdoca>-buzei    is initial and
43                      <ls_acdoca>-co_buzei is initial and
44                      <ls_acdoca>-awitem   is initial.
45                     delete ct_acdoca index lv_tabix.
46                     cs_doc-gl_no_of_lines_excluded += 1.
47                     continue.
48                   endif.
49                 endif.
50               endif.
51             endif.
52           endif.
53
54           determine_jv_amnts_in_acd_line( changing cs_acdoca = <ls_acdoca> ).
55
56           if mv_delete_zero_amount_items = abap_true.
57             if <ls_acdoca>-wsl    = 0 and
58                <ls_acdoca>-hsl    = 0 and
59                <ls_acdoca>-ksl    = 0 and
60                <ls_acdoca>-hsl_4c = 0 and
61                <ls_acdoca>-ksl_4c = 0.
62               delete ct_acdoca index lv_tabix.
63               continue.
64             endif.
65           endif.
66
67           prepare_acdoca_balances( exporting
68                                      is_acdoca   = <ls_acdoca>
69                                    changing
70                                      cs_balances = cs_balances ).
71
72         endloop.
73
74       endmethod.

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Method           CL_JVA_MIG_CORRECT_ACDOCA=====CM01L       Level 1    Page 51
Object           CL_JVA_MIG_CORRECT_ACDOCA                 Lines 357
System           OCQ / 500   User SAP_ABAP   19.10.2026 14:49:37
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
1        method retrieve_and_process_data.
2
3          data: lt_doc_all        type gty_t_doc,
4                lt_doc            type gty_t_doc,
5                lt_doc_fi         type gty_t_doc,
6                lt_doc_co         type gty_t_doc,
7                lt_doc_4a         type gty_t_doc,
8                lt_doc_4b         type gty_t_doc,
9                ls_doc_next       type gty_s_doc,
10               lt_jvso           type jvso_t,
11               lt_jvso_fi        type jvso_t,
12               lt_jvso_4a        type jvso_t,
13               lt_jvso_4b        type jvso_t,
14               ls_item_info      type cl_jva_mig_alv_output=>if_jva_mig_alv_output~gty_s_item_info,
15               lv_doc_count_fico type i,
16               lv_doc_count_me   type i,
17               lv_doc_count_op   type i,
18               lv_doc_count      type i,
19               lt_acdoca         type acdoca_jv_t,
20               lt_acdoca_int     type acdoca_jv_t,
21               lt_acdoca_mig     type acdoca_jv_t,
22               lt_ref            type gty_t_ref,
23               lt_corr_log       type gty_t_corr_log,
24               lt_corr_log_4b    type gty_t_corr_log,
25               lv_last_bulk      type abap_bool.
26
27         "Get all JV documents according to the selection parameters
28         "In the first step the lines are not retrieved, but only the
29         "document numbers and some basic data
30
31         do.
32
33           if ms_progpars-update_fagl_split = abap_true.
34             "lt_open_items = retrieve_open_items( ).
35             "lt_doc_all = retrieve_open_jv_items( ).
36           else.
37             lt_doc_all = retrieve_jv_documents( changing
38                                                   cv_doc_count_fico = lv_doc_count_fico
39                                                   cv_doc_count_me   = lv_doc_count_me
40                                                   cv_doc_count_op   = lv_doc_count_op
41                                                   cv_last_bulk      = lv_last_bulk ).
42           endif.
43
44           rv_count = lines( lt_doc_all ).
45           if rv_count = 0.
46             exit.
47           endif.
48
49           "Process the JV documents in bulks
50           loop at lt_doc_all into data(ls_doc).
51
52             data(lv_tabix) = sy-tabix.
53
54             if ms_progpars-select_fi_documents = abap_true or
55                ms_progpars-select_co_documents = abap_true.
56
57               "Ignore documents where the relevant reference number is missing
58               if ls_doc-activ = gc_jvlinetype-fi_doc   or
59                  ls_doc-activ = gc_jvlinetype-fi_doc_mm.
60                 if ls_doc-reffidoc is initial.
61                   continue.
62                 endif.
63               elseif ls_doc-activ = gc_jvlinetype-co_doc.
64                 if ls_doc-refdocnr is initial.
65                   continue.
66                 endif.
67               endif.
68
69               ms_results-doc_count += 1.
70
71               "If the next JV doc has the same reference data, do not
72               "count it as an extra document
73               clear ls_doc_next.
74               read table lt_doc_all into ls_doc_next index lv_tabix + 1.
75               case ls_doc-activ.
76                 when gc_jvlinetype-fi_doc or gc_jvlinetype-fi_doc_mm.
77                   if ls_doc-activ    <> ls_doc_next-activ or
78                      ls_doc-ryear    <> ls_doc_next-ryear or
79                      ls_doc-reffidoc <> ls_doc_next-reffidoc.
80                     lv_doc_count += 1.
81                   endif.
82                 when gc_jvlinetype-co_doc.
83                   if ls_doc-activ    <> ls_doc_next-activ or
84                      ls_doc-ryear    <> ls_doc_next-ryear or
85                      ls_doc-refdocnr <> ls_doc_next-refdocnr.
86                     lv_doc_count += 1.
87                   endif.
88                 when others.
89                   if ls_doc-activ    <> ls_doc_next-activ or
90                      ls_doc-ryear    <> ls_doc_next-ryear or
91                      ls_doc-reffidoc <> ls_doc_next-reffidoc or
92                      ls_doc-refdocnr <> ls_doc_next-refdocnr.
93                     lv_doc_count += 1.
94                   endif.
95               endcase.
96
97             else.
98
99               "Process every internal JV document
100              lv_doc_count += 1.
101
102              ms_results-doc_count += 1.
103
104            endif.
105
106            if ms_progpars-select_fi_documents = abap_true or
107               ms_progpars-select_co_documents = abap_true.
108              if ls_doc-activ = gc_jvlinetype-co_doc.
109                ls_doc-is_co_doc = abap_true.
110                append ls_doc to lt_doc_co.
111                if ls_doc-reffidoc is not initial.
112                  append ls_doc to lt_doc_fi.
113                endif.
114              else.
115                append ls_doc to lt_doc_fi.
116              endif.
117            else.
118              if ls_doc-rldnr = '4A'.
119                if ms_progpars-newgl_integration_active = abap_true.
120                  append ls_doc to lt_doc_fi.

311              else.

313              endif.
314
315              sort lt_jvso by rbukrs ryear docnr docln.
316
317              sort lt_acdoca by rldnr rbukrs gjahr belnr buzei co_buzei.
318
319              sort lt_acdoca_int by rldnr rbukrs gjahr prec_awtyp prec_aworg prec_awref prec_awitem belnr docln.

321              sort lt_acdoca_mig by rldnr rbukrs gjahr awref awitem jvactivity bttype belnr docln.

327                                               it_acdoca_int = lt_acdoca_int

329                                               it_corr_log   = lt_corr_log
330                                             changing
331                                               ct_doc_jv     = lt_doc ).
332
333              clear lv_doc_count.
334              clear: lt_doc,
335                     lt_doc_fi,
336                     lt_doc_co,
337                     lt_doc_4b,
338                     lt_jvso,
339                     lt_jvso_4b,
340                     lt_ref,
341                     lt_acdoca,
342                     lt_acdoca_int,
343                     lt_acdoca_mig,
344                     lt_corr_log,
345                     lt_corr_log_4b.
346
347            endif.
348
349          endloop.
350
351          if lv_last_bulk = abap_true.
352            exit.
353          endif.

355        enddo.

357      endmethod.

385                  data(lv_message_text) = conv string( 'Document OK - Line assignment failures occurred, but the balances match'(blm) ).
386                else.
387                  lv_message_text = conv string( 'Document OK - No issues detected'(dok) ).
388                endif.
389
390                if ms_progpars-show_all_lines = abap_true or
391                   ls_doc-balances_correct = abap_true.
392
393                  add_item_info_for_doc(
394                      iv_update     = abap_false
395                      is_doc        = ls_doc
396                      it_doc_comb   = lt_doc_comb
397                      it_log        = lt_log_doc
398                      it_acdoca_doc = lt_acdoca_doc
399                      iv_msg_text   = lv_message_text
400                      iv_msg_type   = gc_error_type-info ).
401                else.
402                  loop at lt_log_doc transporting no fields
403                                     where error = gc_error_type-error
404                                        or error = gc_error_type-abend
405                                        or error = gc_error_type-shortdump.
406                    exit.
407                  endloop.
408                  if sy-subrc = 0.
409                    loop at lt_log_doc into ls_log.
410                      add_item_info_for_line( iv_update = abap_false
411                                              is_log    = ls_log ).
412                    endloop.
413                  endif.
414                endif.
415
416            endcase.
417
418          endloop.
419
420          clear lt_doc_comb.
421
422        endloop.
423
424      endmethod.

1        method compare_balances.
2
3          data: lv_balance_error type abap_bool.
4
5          field-symbols: <ls_jvso>   type gty_s_balances_jvso,
6                         <ls_acdoca> type gty_s_balances_acdoca.
7
8          if ( ( cs_doc-activ = gc_jvlinetype-fi_doc or
9                 cs_doc-activ = gc_jvlinetype-fi_doc_mm ) and
10              ms_progpars-check_co_in_bs_lines = abap_true ) or
11            ( cs_doc-activ = gc_jvlinetype-co_doc and
12              ms_progpars-check_co_in_co_lines = abap_true ).
13
14           if cs_balances-t_acdoca_jv_co is initial or
15              cs_balances-t_jvso_jv_co   is initial.
16             return.
17           endif.
18

20                 cs_balances-t_jvso_jv_co.
21
22           "Check if the JV balances can be found in the GL balances
23           loop at cs_balances-t_jvso_jv_co assigning <ls_jvso>.
24             unassign <ls_acdoca>.

26                        with key racct      = <ls_jvso>-racct
27                                 vname      = <ls_jvso>-rjvnam
28                                 egrup      = <ls_jvso>-regrou
29                                 vptnr      = <ls_jvso>-rpartn

35                                 ps_psp_pnr = <ls_jvso>-rprojk
36                                 nplnr      = <ls_jvso>-nplnr
37                        binary search.

39               lv_balance_error = abap_true.
40             else.

42               <ls_jvso>-matched = abap_true.

44                  <ls_jvso>-hsl    = <ls_acdoca>-hsl    and

49                 <ls_jvso>-identical = abap_true.
50               else.
51                 lv_balance_error = abap_true.
52                 add_bal_message_to_log( exporting
53                                           is_jvso_bal   = <ls_jvso>
54                                           is_acdoca_bal = <ls_acdoca>
55                                           with_co_data  = abap_true
56                                         changing
57                                           cs_doc        = cs_doc ).
58               endif.
59             endif.
60           endloop.
61
62           "Check if the remaining GL balances can be found in the JV balances
63           loop at cs_balances-t_acdoca_jv_co assigning <ls_acdoca>
64                                              where matched = abap_false.
65             unassign <ls_jvso>.
66             read table cs_balances-t_jvso_jv_co assigning <ls_jvso>
67                        with key racct   = <ls_acdoca>-racct
68                                 rjvnam  = <ls_acdoca>-vname

70                                 rpartn  = <ls_acdoca>-vptnr

75                                 rordnr  = <ls_acdoca>-aufnr
76                                 rprojk  = <ls_acdoca>-ps_psp_pnr
77                                 nplnr   = <ls_acdoca>-nplnr
78                        binary search.
79             if <ls_jvso> is not assigned.
80               lv_balance_error = abap_true.
81             else.
82               <ls_acdoca>-matched = abap_true.
83               <ls_jvso>-matched = abap_true.
84               if <ls_acdoca>-wsl    = <ls_jvso>-tsl    and
85                  <ls_acdoca>-hsl    = <ls_jvso>-hsl    and
86                  <ls_acdoca>-ksl    = <ls_jvso>-ksl    and

96                                           with_co_data  = abap_true
97                                         changing
98                                           cs_doc        = cs_doc ).
99               endif.
100            endif.
101          endloop.
102
103          if lv_balance_error = abap_true.
104            loop at cs_balances-t_jvso_jv_co assigning <ls_jvso>
105                                             where matched = abap_false.
106              add_bal_message_to_log( exporting

117                                      changing
118                                        cs_doc        = cs_doc ).
119            endloop.
120          endif.
121
122        else.
123
124          if cs_balances-t_acdoca_jv is initial or
125             cs_balances-t_jvso_jv   is initial.
126            return.
127          endif.

130                cs_balances-t_jvso_jv.

145              lv_balance_error = abap_true.

150                 <ls_jvso>-hsl    = <ls_acdoca>-hsl    and
151                 <ls_jvso>-ksl    = <ls_acdoca>-ksl    and

1        method retrieve_fi_doc_header_data.
2
3          data: ls_cobk type cobk.
4
5          "Get the FI document headers
6          select bukrs, gjahr, belnr, awkey, glvor, tcode, bstat, bktxt
7                 from bkpf
8                 into table @data(lt_bkpf)
9                 for all entries in @ct_doc_fi
10                where bukrs = @ms_progpars-bukrs
11                  and gjahr = @ct_doc_fi-ryear
12                  and belnr = @ct_doc_fi-reffidoc
13                order by primary key.
14
15         "Get the CO document headers
16         select kokrs, gjahr, belnr
17                from cobk
18                into table @data(lt_cobk)
19                for all entries in @ct_doc_fi
20                where kokrs = @ct_doc_fi-rkokrs
21                  and gjahr = @ct_doc_fi-ryear
22                  and belnr = @ct_doc_fi-refdocnr
23                order by primary key.
24
25         if lt_cobk[] is initial and
26            lt_bkpf[] is initial.
27           return.
28         endif.
29
30         sort lt_cobk by kokrs gjahr belnr.
31         sort ct_doc_fi by ryear reffidoc.
32         sort ct_doc_co by ryear reffidoc.
33
34         loop at lt_bkpf into data(ls_bkpf).
35
36           read table ct_doc_fi transporting no fields
37                               with key ryear    = ls_bkpf-gjahr
38                                        reffidoc = ls_bkpf-belnr
39                               binary search.
40           if sy-subrc <> 0.
41             continue.
42           endif.
43
44           loop at ct_doc_fi assigning field-symbol(<ls_doc_fi>)
45                             from sy-tabix.
46
47             if <ls_doc_fi>-ryear    <> ls_bkpf-gjahr or
48                <ls_doc_fi>-reffidoc <> ls_bkpf-belnr.
49               exit.
50             endif.
51
52             data(lv_tabix_doc) = sy-tabix.
53
54             move-corresponding ls_bkpf to <ls_doc_fi>.
55
56             "Special handling for internal JV documents
57             if ls_bkpf-tcode(4) = 'GJEC'   or
58                ls_bkpf-tcode(4) = 'GJ19'   or
59                ls_bkpf-tcode(4) = 'GJ17'   or
60                ls_bkpf-tcode(6) = 'GJFARM' or
61                ls_bkpf-bktxt(4) = 'GJEC'   or
62                ls_bkpf-bktxt(4) = 'GJ19'   or
63                ls_bkpf-bktxt(4) = 'GJ17'   or
64                ls_bkpf-bktxt(6) = 'GJFARM'.
65
66               if <ls_doc_fi>-activ = gc_jvlinetype-fi_doc    or
67                  <ls_doc_fi>-activ = gc_jvlinetype-fi_doc_mm.
68
69                 "Do not process internal JV documents as normal FI docs
70                 delete ct_doc_fi index lv_tabix_doc.
71
72               elseif <ls_doc_fi>-activ <> gc_jvlinetype-co_doc.
73
74                 clear ls_cobk.
75
76                 "Check if the JV document was created for a CO document
77                 if <ls_doc_fi>-rkokrs is initial.
78                   "Get the controlling area if it is missing
79                   select single kokrs
80                          from tka02
81                          where bukrs = @<ls_doc_fi>-rbukrs
82                            and gsber is initial
83                          into @<ls_doc_fi>-rkokrs.
84                   "Get the CO document header
85                   select single kokrs, gjahr, belnr
86                          from cobk
87                          where kokrs = @<ls_doc_fi>-rkokrs
88                            and gjahr = @<ls_doc_fi>-ryear
89                            and belnr = @<ls_doc_fi>-refdocnr
90                          into @ls_cobk.
91                   if sy-subrc = 0.
92                     append ls_cobk to lt_cobk.
93                     sort lt_cobk by kokrs gjahr belnr.
94                   endif.
95                 else.
96                   read table lt_cobk into ls_cobk
97                                      with key kokrs = <ls_doc_fi>-rkokrs
98                                               gjahr = <ls_doc_fi>-ryear
99                                               belnr = <ls_doc_fi>-refdocnr.
100                endif.
101                if ls_cobk is initial.
102                  <ls_doc_fi>-is_fi_doc = abap_true.
103                endif.
104
105              endif.
106
107            else.
108
109              if <ls_doc_fi>-activ = gc_jvlinetype-fi_doc    or
110                 <ls_doc_fi>-activ = gc_jvlinetype-fi_doc_mm.
111
112                <ls_doc_fi>-is_fi_doc = abap_true.
113
114              elseif <ls_doc_fi>-is_co_doc = abap_true.
115
116                if ls_bkpf-glvor = 'COFI'.
117
118                  "The REFFIDOC values is wrong
119                  delete ct_doc_fi index lv_tabix_doc.
120
121                else.
122
123                  <ls_doc_fi>-is_fi_doc = abap_true.
124                  <ls_doc_fi>-is_co_doc = abap_false.
125
126                  "Delete the header item from the CO doc table
127                  read table ct_doc_co into data(ls_doc_co)
128                                       with key ryear    = <ls_doc_fi>-ryear
129                                                reffidoc = <ls_doc_fi>-reffidoc.
130                  if sy-subrc = 0.
131                    delete ct_doc_co where ryear    = ls_doc_co-ryear
132                                       and reffidoc = <ls_doc_fi>-reffidoc.
133                  endif.
134
135                endif.
136
137              endif.
138
139            endif.
140
141          endloop.
142
143        endloop.
144
145      endmethod.

1        method retrieve_jv_documents.
2
3          data: lt_doc_op           like rt_doc,
4                lv_max_lines        type i,
5                lv_cond             type string,
6                lv_line_count       type i,
7                lv_line_count_me    type i,
8                lv_line_count_op    type i,
9                lt_period_condition type gty_t_where_condition.
10
11         if ms_progpars-is_parallel_task = abap_true.
12     *      loop at ms_progpars-s_task_package-t_period_condition assigning field-symbol(<ls_period_condition>).
13     *        data(ls_period_condition) = cl_abap_dyn_prg=>escape_quotes( <ls_period_condition> ).
14     *        append ls_period_condition to lt_period_condition.
15     *      endloop.
16
17           loop at ms_progpars-s_task_package-t_periods into data(ls_periods).
18
19             if lt_period_condition[] is not initial.
20               lv_cond = ` OR`.
21             else.
22               clear lv_cond.
23             endif.
24
25             lv_cond &&= ` ryear = ` && ls_periods-ryear ##NO_TEXT.
26
27             lv_cond &&= ` AND (`.
28             loop at ls_periods-t_poper into data(lv_poper).
29               if sy-tabix > 1.
30                 lv_cond &&= ` OR`.
31               endif.
32               lv_cond &&= ` poper = '` && lv_poper && `'`  ##NO_TEXT.
33             endloop.
34             lv_cond &&= ` )`.
35
36             append lv_cond to lt_period_condition.
37
38           endloop.
39
40           insert `(` into lt_period_condition index 1.
41           append `)` to lt_period_condition.
42
43         endif.
44
45         if lt_period_condition[] is initial.
46           lt_period_condition = value #(
47                                          ( ' ( ryear in @ms_progpars-r_ryear' )
48                                          ( '   AND poper in @ms_progpars-r_poper )' )
49                                        )  ##NO_TEXT.
50         endif.
51
52         if ms_progpars-select_fi_documents = abap_true or
53            ms_progpars-select_co_documents = abap_true.
54
55           data(lt_ref_cond) = make_reference_condition( ms_progpars ).
56
57           select distinct rldnr, rbukrs, ryear, docnr, reffidoc, refdocnr, activ
58                           from jvso1
59                           where rldnr     = '4A'
60                             and rbukrs    = @ms_progpars-bukrs
61                             and (lt_period_condition)
62                             and (lt_ref_cond)
63                             and budat    in @ms_progpars-r_budat
64                             and bldat    in @ms_progpars-r_bldat
65                             and racct    in @ms_progpars-r_racct
66                             and rjvnam   in @ms_progpars-r_rjvnam
67                             and regrou   in @ms_progpars-r_regrou
68                             and rrecin   in @ms_progpars-r_rrecin
69                             and bilid    in @ms_progpars-r_bilid
70                             and prodper  in @ms_progpars-r_prodper
71                             and rcntr    in @ms_progpars-r_rcntr
72                             and rordnr   in @ms_progpars-r_rordnr
73                             and rprojk   in @ms_progpars-r_rprojk
74                             and nplnr    in @ms_progpars-r_nplnr
75                             and aufpl    in @ms_progpars-r_aufpl
76                             and aplzl    in @ms_progpars-r_aplzl
77                           order by activ, rbukrs, ryear, reffidoc, refdocnr, docnr
78                           into corresponding fields of table @rt_doc
79                           up to @ms_progpars-bulk_no_of_doc_headers_in_mem rows
80                           offset @cv_doc_count_fico.
81
82           lv_line_count = lines( rt_doc ).
83           cv_doc_count_fico += lv_line_count.
84
85         else.
86
87           if ms_progpars-r_activ_me[] is not initial.
88             select distinct rldnr, rbukrs, ryear, docnr
89                             from jvso1
90                             where rldnr  = '4A'
91                               and rbukrs = @ms_progpars-bukrs
92                               and (lt_period_condition)
93                               and docnr in @ms_progpars-r_docnr
94                               and activ in @ms_progpars-r_activ_me
95                             order by rbukrs, ryear, docnr
96                             into corresponding fields of table @rt_doc
97                             up to @ms_progpars-bulk_no_of_doc_headers_in_mem rows
98                             offset @cv_doc_count_me.
99
100            lv_line_count_me = lines( rt_doc ).
101            cv_doc_count_me += lv_line_count_me.
102          endif.
103
104          if ms_progpars-r_activ_op[] is not initial.
105            lv_max_lines = ms_progpars-bulk_no_of_doc_headers_in_mem - lv_line_count_me.
106
107            if lv_max_lines > 0.
108              select distinct rldnr, rbukrs, ryear, docnr
109                              from jvso2
110                              where rldnr   = '4B'
111                                and rbukrs  = @ms_progpars-bukrs
112                                and (lt_period_condition)
113                                and docnr  in @ms_progpars-r_docnr
114                                and activ  in @ms_progpars-r_activ_op
115                              order by rbukrs, ryear, docnr
116                              into corresponding fields of table @lt_doc_op
117                              up to @lv_max_lines rows
118                              offset @cv_doc_count_op.      "#EC CI_NOFIELD
119
120              lv_line_count_op = lines( lt_doc_op ).
121              cv_doc_count_op += lv_line_count_op.
122
123              append lines of lt_doc_op to rt_doc.
124            endif.
125          endif.
126
127          lv_line_count = lv_line_count_me + lv_line_count_op.
128
129        endif.
130
131        if lv_line_count < ms_progpars-bulk_no_of_doc_headers_in_mem.
132          cv_last_bulk = abap_true.
133        endif.
134
135      endmethod.                    "retrieve_jv_documents

1        method prepare_acdoca_balances.
2
3          data: ls_bal      type gty_s_balances_acdoca,
4                lv_last_day type sy-datum.
5
6          ls_bal-racct   = is_acdoca-racct.
7
8          if ms_progpars-check_venture = abap_true  or
9             ms_progpars-check_jva_data = abap_true.
10           ls_bal-vname   = is_acdoca-vname.
11         endif.
12         if ms_progpars-check_equity_group = abap_true or
13            ms_progpars-check_jva_data = abap_true.
14           ls_bal-egrup   = is_acdoca-egrup.
15         endif.
16         if ms_progpars-check_venture_partner = abap_true or
17            ms_progpars-check_jva_data = abap_true.
18           ls_bal-vptnr   = is_acdoca-vptnr.
19         endif.
20         if ms_progpars-check_recovery_indicator = abap_true or
21            ms_progpars-check_jva_data = abap_true.
22           ls_bal-recid   = is_acdoca-recid.
23         endif.
24         if ms_progpars-check_billing_indicator = abap_true or
25            ms_progpars-check_jva_data = abap_true.
26           ls_bal-btype   = is_acdoca-btype.
27         endif.
28
29         if ( ms_progpars-check_production_period = abap_true or
30              ms_progpars-check_jva_data = abap_true )           and
31            mo_settings->ms_t8jz-pm_act = abap_true.
32           if ms_progpars-prodper_as_period = abap_true.
33             ls_bal-prodper = is_acdoca-prodper(6).
34           elseif ms_progpars-prodper_last_eq_first = abap_true.
35             call function 'LAST_DAY_OF_MONTHS'
36               exporting
37                 day_in            = is_acdoca-prodper
38               importing
39                 last_day_of_month = lv_last_day
40               exceptions
41                 day_in_no_date    = 1
42                 others            = 2.
43             if sy-subrc = 0.
44               if is_acdoca-prodper = lv_last_day.
45                 ls_bal-prodper = is_acdoca-prodper(6) && '01'.
46               endif.
47             else.
48               ls_bal-prodper = is_acdoca-prodper.
49             endif.
50           else.
51             ls_bal-prodper = is_acdoca-prodper.
52           endif.
53         endif.
54
55         ls_bal-wsl    = is_acdoca-wsl.
56         ls_bal-hsl    = is_acdoca-hsl.
57         ls_bal-ksl    = is_acdoca-ksl.
58         ls_bal-hsl_4c = is_acdoca-hsl_4c.
59         ls_bal-ksl_4c = is_acdoca-ksl_4c.
60
61         collect ls_bal into cs_balances-t_acdoca_jv.
62
63         ls_bal-rcntr      = is_acdoca-rcntr.
64         ls_bal-aufnr      = is_acdoca-aufnr.
65         ls_bal-ps_psp_pnr = is_acdoca-ps_psp_pnr.
66         ls_bal-nplnr      = is_acdoca-nplnr.
67
68         collect ls_bal into cs_balances-t_acdoca_jv_co.
69
70       endmethod.

Object           CL_JVA_MIG_CORRECT_ACDOCA                 Lines 163
