1        method retrieve_acdoca_lines.
2
3          data: lt_cond_mig    type gty_t_where_condition,
4                lv_ledger_cond type string.
5
6          if mt_fields_acdoca[] is initial.
7            cl_jva_util=>get_selection_fields(
8              exporting
9                it_table        = ct_acdoca
10               iv_source_table = 'ACDOCA'
11               iv_aggr_amnts   = abap_false
12               iv_prefix       = space
13             changing
14               ct_sel_fields   = mt_fields_acdoca
15               ct_group_fields = mt_group_acdoca ).
16         endif.
17
18         if ms_progpars-correct_all_ledgers = abap_true.
19           lv_ledger_cond = 'rldnr in @mr_ledgers'.
20         else.
21           lv_ledger_cond = 'rldnr = @ms_progpars-jv_ledger'.
22         endif.
23
24         if mv_has_migrated_docs = abap_true.
25           if mv_has_migrated_jv_docs = abap_true.
26             "Do only retrieve items that were not migrated or where the venture is filled
27             lt_cond_mig = value #(
28                                    ( ' ( mig_source is initial OR ' )
29                                    ( '   vname is not initial )' )
30                                  ).
31           else.
32             "Exclude the totals corrections
33             lt_cond_mig = value #(
34                                    ( ' ( mig_source <> ''S'' AND ' )
35                                    ( '   mig_source <> ''T'' AND ' )
36                                    ( '   mig_source <> ''U'' AND ' )
37                                    ( '   mig_source <> ''V'' )' )
38                                  ).
39           endif.
40         endif.
41
42         case iv_type.
43
44           when 'FI'.
45
46             if it_doc[] is not initial.
47               select (mt_fields_acdoca) from acdoca
48                                         for all entries in @it_doc
49                                         where (lv_ledger_cond)
50                                           and rbukrs = @ms_progpars-bukrs
51                                           and gjahr  = @it_doc-ryear
52                                           and belnr  = @it_doc-reffidoc
53                                           and (lt_cond_mig)
54                                         appending corresponding fields of table @ct_acdoca.
55             endif.
56
57           when 'CO'.
58
59             if it_doc[] is not initial.
60               select (mt_fields_acdoca) from acdoca
61                                         for all entries in @it_doc
62                                         where (lv_ledger_cond)
63                                           and rbukrs   = @ms_progpars-bukrs
64                                           and gjahr    = @it_doc-ryear
65                                           and co_belnr = @it_doc-refdocnr
66                                           and (lt_cond_mig)
67                                         appending corresponding fields of table @ct_acdoca.
68             endif.
69
70           when '4A'.
71
72             if it_doc[] is not initial.
73               select (mt_fields_acdoca) from acdoca
74                                         for all entries in @it_doc
75                                         where (lv_ledger_cond)
76                                           and rbukrs = @ms_progpars-bukrs
77                                           and gjahr  = @it_doc-ryear
78                                           and awtyp  = 'BKPFF'
79                                           and zuonr  = @it_doc-zuonr
80                                         appending corresponding fields of table @ct_acdoca.
81             endif.
82
83           when '4B'.
84
85             if it_doc[] is not initial.
86               select (mt_fields_acdoca) from acdoca
87                                         for all entries in @it_doc
88                                         where (lv_ledger_cond)
89                                           and rbukrs = @ms_progpars-bukrs
90                                           and gjahr  = @it_doc-ryear
91                                           and awtyp  = 'BKPFF'
92                                           and zuonr  = @it_doc-zuonr
93                                         appending corresponding fields of table @ct_acdoca.
94             endif.
95
96           when 'IN'.
97
98             if it_ref[] is not initial.
99               "Retrieve the documents that were posted via ACDOCA-only posting functionality
100              select (mt_fields_acdoca) from acdoca
101                                        for all entries in @it_ref
102                                        where (lv_ledger_cond)
103                                          and rbukrs      = @ms_progpars-bukrs
104                                          and gjahr       = @it_ref-ryear
105                                          and vorgn       = 'RFRA'
106                                          and ( prec_awtyp  = @it_ref-awtyp or
107                                                prec_awtyp  is initial )
108                                          and ( prec_aworg  = @it_ref-aworg or
109                                                prec_aworg  is initial )
110                                          and prec_awref  = @it_ref-refdocnr
111                                          and prec_awitem = @it_ref-refdocln
112                                        appending corresponding fields of table @ct_acdoca.
113            endif.
114
115          when 'MI'.
116
117            "Special handling for documents migrated during S4HANA conversion
118            "If a document is in the JV ledger only, but not in FI,
119            "it should exist with mig_source = 'J' and/or awtyp = 'JVAME'/'JVAM'
120
121            if it_doc[] is not initial.
122              select (mt_fields_acdoca) from acdoca
123                                        for all entries in @it_doc
124                                        where (lv_ledger_cond)
125                                          and rbukrs = @ms_progpars-bukrs
126                                          and gjahr  = @it_doc-ryear
127                                          and ( mig_source = 'J' or
128                                                awtyp  = 'JVAME' or
129                                                awtyp  = 'JVAM' )
130                                          and awref  = @it_doc-refdocnr
131                                        appending corresponding fields of table @ct_acdoca.
132            endif.
133
134            if it_ref[] is not initial.
135              select (mt_fields_acdoca) from acdoca
136                                        for all entries in @it_ref
137                                        where (lv_ledger_cond)
138                                          and rbukrs = @ms_progpars-bukrs
139                                          and gjahr  = @it_ref-ryear
140                                          and ( mig_source = 'J' or
141                                                awtyp  = 'JVAME' or
142                                                awtyp  = 'JVAM' )
143                                          and awref  = @it_ref-refdocnr
144                                          and jvactivity is not initial
145                                        appending corresponding fields of table @ct_acdoca.
146            endif.
147
148          when 'LG'.
149
150            if it_corr_log[] is not initial.
151              "Retrieve the documents that were posted through this program in a different fiscal year
152              select (mt_fields_acdoca) from acdoca
153                                        for all entries in @it_corr_log
154                                        where (lv_ledger_cond)
155                                          and rbukrs      = @ms_progpars-bukrs
156                                          and gjahr       = @it_corr_log-gjahr_new
157                                          and belnr       = @it_corr_log-belnr_new
158                                        appending corresponding fields of table @ct_acdoca.
159            endif.
160
161        endcase.
162
163      endmethod.

1        method combine_and_analyze_jv_lines.
2
3          data: ls_jvso_comb  type jvso,
4                lv_docnr_last type jvso,
5                ls_ref        type gty_s_ref,
6                ls_ref_last   type gty_s_ref,
7                lv_tabix_new  type sy-tabix.
8
9          field-symbols: <ls_doc> type gty_s_doc.
10
11         clear: et_jvso_combined,
12                et_ref.
13
14         loop at ct_jvso assigning field-symbol(<ls_jvso>).
15
16           if <ls_jvso>-rldnr = '4A'.
17
18             clear ls_ref.
19             move-corresponding <ls_jvso> to ls_ref.
20             if ls_ref <> ls_ref_last.
21               ls_ref_last = ls_ref.
22               collect ls_ref into et_ref.
23             endif.
24
25             if <ls_jvso>-docnr <> lv_docnr_last.
26               lv_docnr_last = <ls_jvso>-docnr.
27               unassign <ls_doc>.
28               read table ct_doc assigning <ls_doc>
29                                 with key ryear = <ls_jvso>-ryear
30                                          docnr = <ls_jvso>-docnr
31                                 binary search.
32             endif.
33
34             if <ls_doc> is assigned.
35               if <ls_doc>-rkokrs is initial.
36                 if <ls_jvso>-rkokrs is not initial.
37                   <ls_doc>-rkokrs = <ls_jvso>-rkokrs.
38                 endif.
39               endif.
40               if <ls_jvso>-activ <> gc_jvlinetype-balancing_line.
41                 if <ls_doc>-activ is initial.
42                   if <ls_jvso>-activ is not initial.
43                     <ls_doc>-activ = <ls_jvso>-activ.
44                   endif.
45                 endif.
46                 if <ls_jvso>-reffidoc is not initial.
47                   if <ls_jvso>-refactiv = gc_jvdoctype-co_doc.
48                     clear <ls_jvso>-reffidoc.
49                   else.
50                     if <ls_doc>-reffidoc is initial.
51                       <ls_doc>-reffidoc = <ls_jvso>-reffidoc.
52                     elseif <ls_doc>-reffidoc <> <ls_jvso>-reffidoc.
53                       clear <ls_doc>-reffidoc.
54                     endif.
55                   endif.
56                 endif.
57                 if <ls_jvso>-refdocnr is not initial.
58                   if <ls_doc>-refdocnr is initial.
59                     <ls_doc>-refdocnr = <ls_jvso>-refdocnr.
60                   elseif <ls_doc>-refdocnr <> <ls_jvso>-refdocnr.
61                     clear <ls_doc>-refdocnr.
62                   endif.
63                 endif.
64               endif.
65             endif.
66
67           endif.
68
69           if <ls_jvso>-rldnr = '4A' or
70              <ls_jvso>-rldnr = '4B'.
71
72             clear ls_jvso_comb.
73
74             move-corresponding <ls_jvso> to ls_jvso_comb.
75             if <ls_jvso>-rldnr = '4B'.
76               ls_jvso_comb-bilid = <ls_jvso>-rbilid.
77               clear: ls_jvso_comb-refdocnr,
78                      ls_jvso_comb-refdocln.
79             endif.
80             ls_jvso_comb-curt1 = ms_classic_currencies-curt1_jv.
81             ls_jvso_comb-hwaer = ms_classic_currencies-hwaer_jv.
82             if ms_classic_currencies-curt2_jv is initial.
83               clear ls_jvso_comb-ksl.
84             else.
85               ls_jvso_comb-curt2 = ms_classic_currencies-curt2_jv.
86               ls_jvso_comb-hwae2 = ms_classic_currencies-hwae2_jv.
87             endif.
88             append ls_jvso_comb to et_jvso_combined.
89             lv_tabix_new = sy-tabix.
90
91           else.
92
93             if <ls_jvso>-docnr = ls_jvso_comb-docnr and
94                <ls_jvso>-docln = ls_jvso_comb-docln.
95
96               if ms_classic_currencies-curt3_jv is not initial.
97                 ls_jvso_comb-hsl_4c = <ls_jvso>-hsl.
98                 ls_jvso_comb-curt3  = ms_classic_currencies-curt3_jv.
99                 ls_jvso_comb-hwae3  = ms_classic_currencies-hwae3_jv.
100              endif.
101              if ms_classic_currencies-curt4_jv is not initial.
102                ls_jvso_comb-ksl_4c = <ls_jvso>-ksl.
103                ls_jvso_comb-curt4  = ms_classic_currencies-curt4_jv.
104                ls_jvso_comb-hwae4  = ms_classic_currencies-hwae4_jv.
105              endif.
106              modify et_jvso_combined from ls_jvso_comb index lv_tabix_new.
107
108            else.
109
110              clear ls_jvso_comb.
111
112              move-corresponding <ls_jvso> to ls_jvso_comb.
113              if <ls_jvso>-rldnr = '4D'.
114                ls_jvso_comb-bilid = <ls_jvso>-rbilid.
115              endif.
116              clear: ls_jvso_comb-hsl, ls_jvso_comb-ksl.
117              if ms_classic_currencies-curt3_jv is not initial.
118                ls_jvso_comb-hsl_4c = <ls_jvso>-hsl.
119                ls_jvso_comb-curt3  = ms_classic_currencies-curt3_jv.
120                ls_jvso_comb-hwae3  = ms_classic_currencies-hwae3_jv.
121              endif.
122              if ms_classic_currencies-curt4_jv is not initial.
123                ls_jvso_comb-ksl_4c = <ls_jvso>-ksl.
124                ls_jvso_comb-curt4  = ms_classic_currencies-curt4_jv.
125                ls_jvso_comb-hwae4  = ms_classic_currencies-hwae4_jv.
126              endif.
127              append ls_jvso_comb to et_jvso_combined.
128              lv_tabix_new = sy-tabix.
129
130            endif.
131
132          endif.
133
134        endloop.
135
136      endmethod.

1        method retrieve_log_items_diff_years.
2
3          "Get the items from log for which new GL docs were posted
4          "in a different fiscal year (the others will be picked up differently)
5          select * from jva_acd_upd_log
6                   for all entries in @it_jvso
7                   where rbukrs = @it_jvso-rbukrs
8                     and ryear  = @it_jvso-ryear
9                     and docnr  = @it_jvso-docnr
10                    and ( docln = @it_jvso-docln or
11                          docln = @space )
12                    and gjahr_new <> @it_jvso-ryear
13                    and belnr_new is not initial
14                  into table @rt_corr_log.
15
16       endmethod.

1        method get_acdoca_amount_for_jv.
2
3          if iv_curtp is initial.
4            return.
5          endif.
6
7          case iv_curtp.
8
9            when ms_curr_info-curtype1.
10             rv_amount = is_acdoca-hsl.
11
12           when ms_curr_info-curtype2.
13             rv_amount = is_acdoca-ksl.
14
15           when ms_curr_info-curtype3.
16             rv_amount = is_acdoca-osl.
17
18           when ms_curr_info-curtype4.
19             rv_amount = is_acdoca-vsl.
20
21           when ms_curr_info-curtype5.
22             rv_amount = is_acdoca-bsl.
23
24           when ms_curr_info-curtype6.
25             rv_amount = is_acdoca-csl.
26
27           when ms_curr_info-curtype7.
28             rv_amount = is_acdoca-dsl.
29
30           when ms_curr_info-curtype8.
31             rv_amount = is_acdoca-esl.
32
33           when ms_curr_info-curtype9.
34             rv_amount = is_acdoca-fsl.
35
36           when ms_curr_info-curtype10.
37             rv_amount = is_acdoca-gsl.
38
39           when others.
40             if iv_waers = is_acdoca-rwcur.
41               rv_amount = is_acdoca-wsl.
42             endif.
43
44         endcase.
45
46       endmethod.

1        method compare_field_values.
2
3          data: lv_wsl         type acdoca-wsl,
4                lv_hsl         type acdoca-hsl,
5                lv_ksl         type acdoca-ksl,
6                lv_hsl_4c      type acdoca-hsl,
7                lv_ksl_4c      type acdoca-ksl,
8                lv_wsl_rev     type acdoca-wsl,
9                lv_hsl_rev     type acdoca-hsl,
10               lv_ksl_rev     type acdoca-ksl,
11               lv_hsl_4c_rev  type acdoca-hsl,
12               lv_ksl_4c_rev  type acdoca-ksl,
13               lv_issue_type  type c,
14               lv_object_name type char100,
15               lv_is_co_line  type abap_bool,
16               lt_log         type gty_t_log,
17               ls_message     type bapiret2,
18               lv_corr_type   type c,
19               lv_error_type  type c,
20               lv_first_day   type sy-datum,
21               lv_last_day    type sy-datum,
22               ls_cokey       type cokey_is.
23
24         field-symbols: <lv_acdoca_value> type any,
25                        <lv_jvso_value>   type any.
26
27         if is_acdoca-co_buzei is not initial or
28            ( is_acdoca-mig_source is not initial and
29              is_acdoca-glaccount_type = 'P' ).
30           lv_is_co_line = abap_true.
31         endif.
32
33         "Check if the amounts match
34         if cs_log-heuristic_hit = abap_false.
35
36           lv_wsl    = is_jvso-tsl.
37           lv_hsl    = is_jvso-hsl.
38           lv_ksl    = is_jvso-ksl.
39           lv_hsl_4c = is_jvso-hsl_4c.
40           lv_ksl_4c = is_jvso-ksl_4c.
41
42           lv_wsl_rev    = - is_jvso-tsl.
43           lv_hsl_rev    = - is_jvso-hsl.
44           lv_ksl_rev    = - is_jvso-ksl.
45           lv_hsl_4c_rev = - is_jvso-hsl_4c.
46           lv_ksl_4c_rev = - is_jvso-ksl_4c.
47
48           if ( is_acdoca-wsl = lv_wsl_rev and
49                is_acdoca-wsl <> 0  )                or
50              ( is_acdoca-hsl = lv_hsl_rev and
51                is_acdoca-hsl <> 0 )                 or
52              ( is_acdoca-ksl = lv_ksl_rev and
53                is_acdoca-ksl <> 0 )                 or
54              ( is_acdoca-hsl_4c = lv_hsl_4c_rev and
55                is_acdoca-hsl_4c <> 0 )              or
56              ( is_acdoca-ksl_4c = lv_ksl_4c_rev and
57                is_acdoca-ksl_4c <> 0 ).
58             cs_log-issues &&= gc_issue_type-wrong_amount_sign.
59             if iv_jvdoctype = gc_jvdoctype-fi_doc    or
60                iv_jvdoctype = gc_jvdoctype-co_doc    or
61                iv_jvdoctype = gc_jvdoctype-cash_call or
62                iv_jvdoctype = gc_jvdoctype-cutback.
63               cs_log-corr_type = gc_corr_type-correction_not_possible.
64               lv_error_type = gc_error_type-error.
65             else.
66               cs_log-corr_type = gc_corr_type-rev_and_repost_doc.
67               lv_error_type = gc_error_type-warning.
68             endif.
69             if is_acdoca-wsl <> 0 and
70                is_acdoca-wsl = lv_wsl_rev.
71               add_issue_info(
72                 exporting
73                   is_log          = cs_log
74                   is_acdoca       = is_acdoca
75                   iv_object_name  = 'trans. curr. sign'(trs)
76                   iv_issue_type   = lv_issue_type
77                   iv_jvso_value   = is_jvso-tsl
78                   iv_acdoca_value = is_acdoca-wsl
79                   iv_error_type   = lv_error_type
80                 changing
81                   ct_log          = lt_log ).
82             endif.
83             if is_acdoca-hsl <> 0 and
84                is_acdoca-hsl = lv_hsl_rev.
85               add_issue_info(
86                 exporting
87                   is_log          = cs_log
88                   is_acdoca       = is_acdoca
89                   iv_object_name  = 'local curr. sign'(lcs)
90                   iv_issue_type   = lv_issue_type
91                   iv_jvso_value   = is_jvso-hsl
92                   iv_acdoca_value = is_acdoca-hsl
93                   iv_error_type   = lv_error_type
94                 changing
95                   ct_log          = lt_log ).
96             endif.
97             if is_acdoca-ksl <> 0 and
98                is_acdoca-ksl = lv_ksl_rev.
99               add_issue_info(
100                exporting
101                  is_log          = cs_log
102                  is_acdoca       = is_acdoca
103                  iv_object_name  = '2nd local curr. sign'(2cs)
104                  iv_issue_type   = lv_issue_type
105                  iv_jvso_value   = is_jvso-ksl
106                  iv_acdoca_value = is_acdoca-ksl
107                  iv_error_type   = lv_error_type
108                changing
109                  ct_log          = lt_log ).
110            endif.
111            if is_acdoca-hsl_4c <> 0 and
112               is_acdoca-hsl_4c = lv_hsl_4c_rev.
113              add_issue_info(
114                exporting
115                  is_log          = cs_log
116                  is_acdoca       = is_acdoca
117                  iv_object_name  = '3rd local curr. sign'(3cs)
118                  iv_issue_type   = lv_issue_type
119                  iv_jvso_value   = is_jvso-hsl_4c
120                  iv_acdoca_value = is_acdoca-hsl_4c
121                  iv_error_type   = lv_error_type
122                changing
123                  ct_log          = lt_log ).
124            endif.
125            if is_acdoca-ksl_4c <> 0 and
126               is_acdoca-ksl_4c = lv_ksl_4c_rev.
127              add_issue_info(
128                exporting
129                  is_log          = cs_log
130                  is_acdoca       = is_acdoca
131                  iv_object_name  = '4th local curr. sign'(4cs)
132                  iv_issue_type   = lv_issue_type
133                  iv_jvso_value   = is_jvso-ksl_4c
134                  iv_acdoca_value = is_acdoca-ksl_4c
135                  iv_error_type   = lv_error_type
136                changing
137                  ct_log          = lt_log ).
138            endif.
139          elseif is_acdoca-wsl    <> lv_wsl or
140                 is_acdoca-hsl    <> lv_hsl or
141                 is_acdoca-ksl    <> lv_ksl or
142                 is_acdoca-hsl_4c <> lv_hsl_4c or
143                 is_acdoca-ksl_4c <> lv_ksl_4c.
144            cs_log-issues &&= gc_issue_type-wrong_amount.
145            if iv_jvdoctype = gc_jvdoctype-fi_doc    or
146               iv_jvdoctype = gc_jvdoctype-co_doc    or
147               iv_jvdoctype = gc_jvdoctype-cash_call or
148               iv_jvdoctype = gc_jvdoctype-cutback.
149              cs_log-corr_type = gc_corr_type-correction_not_possible.
150              lv_error_type = gc_error_type-error.
151            else.
152              cs_log-corr_type = gc_corr_type-rev_and_repost_doc.
153              lv_error_type = gc_error_type-warning.
154            endif.
155            if is_acdoca-wsl <> lv_wsl.
156              add_issue_info(
157                exporting
158                  is_log          = cs_log
159                  is_acdoca       = is_acdoca
160                  iv_object_name  = 'trans. curr. amnt.'(tra)
161                  iv_issue_type   = lv_issue_type
162                  iv_jvso_value   = is_jvso-tsl
163                  iv_acdoca_value = is_acdoca-wsl
164                  iv_error_type   = lv_error_type
165                changing
166                  ct_log          = lt_log ).
167            endif.
168            if is_acdoca-hsl <> lv_hsl.
169              add_issue_info(
170                exporting

78             ls_log-rldnr_acd = is_doc-ledger.

80             if lv_compressed = abap_true.

89                   ct_jv_compr_info  = lt_jv_compr_info ).

99               endif.

101            endif.

103            lt_acdoca = get_acdoca_line_for_jv(
104                          exporting
105                            is_doc              = is_doc
106                            is_jvso             = ls_jvso
107                            iv_tabix            = lv_tabix
108                            it_acdoca_doc       = ct_acdoca_doc

116                          importing
117                            et_acd_compr_info   = lt_acd_compr_info
118                          changing
119                            cs_log              = ls_log ).

121            if ls_log-error = gc_error_type-error.
122
123              lv_error_count += 1.
124
125              append_log_item_for_jv_line( exporting
126                                             is_log           = ls_log
127                                             is_jvso          = ls_jvso
128                                             it_jv_compr_info = lt_jv_compr_info
129                                           changing
130                                             ct_log_doc       = et_log_doc ).
131
132              if ls_log-corr_type > ev_corr_type.
133                ev_corr_type = ls_log-corr_type.
134              endif.
135
136            elseif lines( lt_acdoca ) = 0.
137
138              lv_lines_missing_count += 1.
139
140              append_log_item_for_jv_line( exporting
141                                             is_log           = ls_log
142                                             is_jvso          = ls_jvso
143                                             it_jv_compr_info = lt_jv_compr_info
144                                           changing
145                                             ct_log_doc       = et_log_doc ).
146
147              if ls_log-corr_type > ev_corr_type.
148                ev_corr_type = ls_log-corr_type.
149              endif.
150
151            else.
152
153              loop at lt_acdoca into data(ls_acdoca).
154
155                data(lv_tabix_acd) = sy-tabix.
156
157                if ct_acdoca_doc[] is initial.
158                  read table lt_acdoca_doc transporting no fields
159                                           with key docln = ls_acdoca-docln
160                                           binary search.
161                  if sy-subrc <> 0.
162                    insert ls_acdoca into lt_acdoca_doc index sy-tabix.
163                  endif.
164                endif.
165
166                data(ls_log_item) = ls_log.
167                clear: ls_log_item-comment,
168                       ls_log_item-messages[].
169
170                ls_log_item-rldnr_acd = ls_acdoca-rldnr.
171                ls_log_item-belnr_acd = ls_acdoca-belnr.
172                ls_log_item-docln_acd = ls_acdoca-docln.
173                ls_log_item-gjahr_acd = ls_acdoca-gjahr.
174
175                compare_field_values(
176                  exporting
177                    is_doc            = is_doc
178                    is_jvso           = ls_jvso
179                    is_acdoca         = ls_acdoca
180                    iv_tabix_acd      = lv_tabix_acd
181                    iv_jvdoctype      = is_doc-jvdoctype
182                    it_jv_compr_info  = lt_jv_compr_info
183                    it_acd_compr_info = lt_acd_compr_info
184                  changing
185                    cs_log            = ls_log_item ).
186
187                lv_error_type_jv_line = set_error_type(
188                                          iv_old_error = lv_error_type_jv_line
189                                          iv_new_error = ls_log_item-error ).
190
191                if ls_log_item-corr_type > lv_corr_type_jv_line.
192                  lv_corr_type_jv_line = ls_log_item-corr_type.
193                endif.
194                if ls_log_item-corr_type > ev_corr_type.
195                  ev_corr_type = ls_log_item-corr_type.
196                endif.

198                append ls_acdoca-docln to lt_acd_docln.
199                append ls_log_item to lt_log.
200
201                ls_acd_jv_assignments-docln = ls_acdoca-docln.
202                if ls_jvso-docln is not initial.
203                  ls_line_info-docnr = ls_jvso-docnr.
204                  ls_line_info-docln = ls_jvso-docln.
205                  append ls_line_info to ls_acd_jv_assignments-t_jv_docln.
206                else.
207                  read table lt_jv_compr_info assigning field-symbol(<ls_jv_compr_info>)
208                                              with key tabix_aggr = ls_jvso-tabix_aggr.
209                  if sy-subrc = 0.
210                    append lines of <ls_jv_compr_info>-t_docln_original to ls_acd_jv_assignments-t_jv_docln.
211                  endif.
212                endif.
213                append ls_acd_jv_assignments to lt_acd_jv_assignments.
214
215              endloop.
216
217              case lv_error_type_jv_line.
218                when gc_error_type-error or
219                     gc_error_type-abend or
220                     gc_error_type-shortdump.
221                  lv_error_count += 1.
222                when gc_error_type-warning.
223                  case ls_log_item-issues.
224                    when gc_issue_type-line_missing.
225                      lv_lines_missing_count += 1.
226                    when others.
227                      lv_lines_to_be_corrected_count += 1.
228                  endcase.
229                when others.
230                  lv_lines_without_issues_count += 1.
231              endcase.
232
233              loop at lt_log into ls_log_item.
234
235                data(lv_add_messages) = abap_false.
236                if lines( ls_log_item-messages ) > 0 and
237                   lines( ls_log-messages ) > 0.
238                  "Check if the same message was already added to ls_log-messages
239                  read table ls_log-messages transporting no fields
240                                             with key message = ls_log_item-messages[ 1 ]-message.
241                  if sy-subrc <> 0.
242                    lv_add_messages = abap_true.
243                  endif.
244                else.
245                  lv_add_messages = abap_true.
246                endif.
247                if lv_add_messages = abap_true.
248                  append lines of ls_log_item-messages to ls_log-messages.
249                endif.
250
251                ls_log-issues = ls_log_item-issues.
252                if ls_log_item-corr_type > ls_log-corr_type.
253                  ls_log-corr_type = ls_log_item-corr_type.
254                endif.
255                if ls_log_item-corr_type = gc_corr_type-correction_not_required.
256                  if ls_log-comment cs ls_log_item-comment.
257                    continue.
258                  endif.
259                  if ls_log-comment is not initial.
260                    ls_log-comment &&= ` - `.
261                  endif.
262                  ls_log-comment &&= ls_log_item-comment.
263                endif.
264
265              endloop.
266
267              append_log_item_for_jv_line( exporting
268                                             is_log            = ls_log
269                                             is_jvso           = ls_jvso
270                                             it_jv_compr_info  = lt_jv_compr_info
271                                           changing
272                                             ct_log_doc        = et_log_doc ).
273
274              "If no critical error occurred during the line check, add the assignments
275              if lv_corr_type_jv_line < gc_corr_type-rev_and_repost_line.
276                ls_jv_acd_assignments-t_acd_docln[] = lt_acd_docln[].
277                if ls_jvso-docln is not initial.

279                  ls_jv_acd_assignments-docln = ls_jvso-docln.
280                  append ls_jv_acd_assignments to es_line_assignments-jv_acd.
281                else.
282                  read table lt_jv_compr_info assigning <ls_jv_compr_info>
283                                              with key tabix_aggr = ls_jvso-tabix_aggr.
284                  if sy-subrc = 0 and <ls_jv_compr_info> is assigned.
285                    loop at <ls_jv_compr_info>-t_docln_original into data(lv_line_info).
286                      ls_jv_acd_assignments-docnr = lv_line_info-docnr.
287                      ls_jv_acd_assignments-docln = lv_line_info-docln.
288                      append ls_jv_acd_assignments to es_line_assignments-jv_acd.
289                    endloop.
290                  endif.
291                endif.
292
293                loop at lt_acd_jv_assignments into ls_acd_jv_assignments.
294                  read table es_line_assignments-acd_jv transporting no fields
295                                                        with key docln = ls_acd_jv_assignments-docln
296                                                        binary search.
297                  insert ls_acd_jv_assignments into es_line_assignments-acd_jv
298                                               index sy-tabix.
299                endloop.
300              endif.
301
302            endif.

304          endloop.
305
306          if ct_acdoca_doc[] is initial.
307            ct_acdoca_doc[] = lt_acdoca_doc[].
308          endif.
309
310          if lines( es_line_assignments-jv_acd ) = lines( lt_jvso ).
311            es_line_assignments-all_lines_assigned = abap_true.
312          endif.
313          sort es_line_assignments-jv_acd by docnr docln.
314
315          if lv_error_count > 0.
316            data(lv_corr_type) = gc_corr_type-correction_not_possible.
317          elseif lv_lines_missing_count = lv_line_count.
318            if is_doc-jvdoctype = gc_jvdoctype-fi_doc or
319               is_doc-jvdoctype = gc_jvdoctype-co_doc.
320              lv_corr_type = gc_corr_type-correction_not_possible.
321            else.
322              lv_corr_type = gc_corr_type-post_new_doc.
323            endif.
324          elseif lv_lines_to_be_reversed_count > 0 or
325                 ( lv_lines_missing_count > 0 and
326                   lv_lines_missing_count < lv_line_count ).
327            if is_doc-jvdoctype = gc_jvdoctype-fi_doc or
328               is_doc-jvdoctype = gc_jvdoctype-co_doc.
329              lv_corr_type = gc_corr_type-correction_not_possible.
330            else.
331              if is_doc-posting_mode = gc_jvposttype-combined.
332                lv_corr_type = gc_corr_type-rev_and_repost_doc.
333              else.
334                clear ev_corr_type.
335                lv_corr_type = gc_corr_type-update_line.
336              endif.
337            endif.
338          endif.
339
340          if lv_corr_type > ev_corr_type.
341            ev_corr_type = lv_corr_type.
342          endif.
343
344          "Check if the new results is better than the old one. If the old
345          "one is better, use the old results for further processing
346          if iv_recursion_level > 0.
347            if it_log_doc_old[] is not initial and
348               iv_error_count_old < lv_error_count.
349              ev_corr_type = iv_corr_type_old.
350              et_log_doc[] = it_log_doc_old[].
351              lv_error_count = iv_error_count_old.
352              es_line_assignments = is_line_assignments_old.
353            endif.
354          endif.
355
356        endif.
357
358        if ms_progpars-heuristic_line_assignment = abap_false   or
359           ( is_doc-activ <> gc_jvlinetype-fi_doc     and
360             is_doc-activ <> gc_jvlinetype-fi_doc_mm )          or
361           ev_corr_type <> gc_corr_type-correction_not_possible or
362           lv_compress_by_pc = abap_true                        or
363           iv_recursion_level >= 3.
364          return.
365        endif.
366
367        compare_lines(
368          exporting
369            is_doc                  = is_doc
370            it_jvso_doc             = it_jvso_doc
371            it_acdoca_all           = it_acdoca_all
372            it_acdoca_int           = it_acdoca_int

1        method check_for_repostings_jv.
2
3          data: lv_latest_cpudt type cpudt,
4                lv_latest_cputm type cputm.
5
6          data(lv_original_line_count) = lines( ct_jvso ).
7
8          "Check if the document was reposted by RGJVREC8
9          "If yes, delete the irrelevant items
10         loop at ct_jvso assigning field-symbol(<ls_jvso>)
11                         where rldnr = '4A'.
12           "and sgtxt+26(6) = 'REPOST'
13           "and sgtxt(8)    = 'RGJVREC8'.
14           if lv_latest_cpudt is initial or
15              lv_latest_cputm is initial or
16              <ls_jvso>-cpudt > lv_latest_cpudt or
17              ( <ls_jvso>-cpudt = lv_latest_cpudt and
18                <ls_jvso>-cputm > lv_latest_cputm ).
19             lv_latest_cpudt = <ls_jvso>-cpudt.
20             lv_latest_cputm = <ls_jvso>-cputm.
21           endif.
22         endloop.
23
24         if lv_latest_cpudt is not initial.
25
26           "Keep only the lines with the latest CPU date and time
27           delete ct_jvso where cpudt <> lv_latest_cpudt
28                             or cputm <> lv_latest_cputm.
29
30           "Check if there is a reposting
31           read table ct_jvso transporting no fields
32                              with key sgtxt(8)    = 'RGJVREC8'
33                                       sgtxt+26(6) = 'REPOST'.
34           if sy-subrc <= 2.
35             "Delete reversal lines
36             delete ct_jvso where sgtxt(8)    =  'RGJVREC8'
37                              and sgtxt+26(6) <> 'REPOST'.
38           endif.
39
40         endif.
41
42         if lv_original_line_count <> lines( ct_jvso ).
43           rv_reposted = abap_true.
44         endif.
45
46       endmethod.

29           "No failed tasks:
30           "Take objects from regular input list of objects
31           read table mt_taskdata into ls_taskdata index 1.
32
33           if sy-subrc = 0.
34
35             ls_taskdata-scheduled = abap_true.
36
37             ls_obj_in_process-obj_id = ls_taskdata-obj_id.
38
39             delete mt_taskdata index 1.
40
41             "Add list of objects that are about to be processed
42             "to list of "objects in process"
43             "so the task manager has that information
44             append ls_obj_in_process to ct_objects_in_process.
45
46           endif.
47
48         endif.
49
50         "If there is (currently) nothing to do, clear the
51         "START_RFC field and leave the form.
52         "This informs the task manager that no rfc has to be started.
53         "If there are no more RFCs in process this also ends
54         "the processing of the task manager
55         "If there are still RFCs in process the BEFORE_RFC form
56         "will be invoked after each RFC has been received to give
57         "the application an opportunity to launch new RFCs that have been
58         "waiting on the RFC that was just received.
59         if ct_objects_in_process is initial.
60           clear cs_before_rfc_exp-start_rfc.
61           return.
62         endif.
63
64         "Convert the input data into the INDX structure
65         "that is needed for the RFC
66         mo_output->spta_indx_package_encode(
67           exporting
68             iv_data    = ls_taskdata
69           importing
70             et_indxtab = ct_rfcdata ).
71
72         "Inform task manager that an RFC can be started from the
73         "data compiled
74         cs_before_rfc_exp-start_rfc = abap_true.
75
76         ms_results_global-runtime_tasks_admin += calc_runtime( iv_timestamp_start = lv_time_stamp_start_task
77                                                                iv_timestamp_end   = get_timestamp( ) ).

79       endmethod.

1        method if_jva_mig_correct_acdoca~parall_in_rfc.

1        method check_cocodes_and_authorities.
2
3          data: lv_process_code    type jv_proca value 'CUTBACK2',
4                lv_activity        type activ_auth,
5                lv_auth_miss_count type i.
6
7          "Get the JV company codes
8          select bukrs from t8jz
9                       where bukrs in @ms_progpars-r_bukrs
10                      into table @ms_progpars-t_bukrs.
11
12         data(lv_jv_cocodes_count) = lines( ms_progpars-t_bukrs ).
13
14         if lv_jv_cocodes_count = 0.
15           rv_error = abap_true.
16           if sy-batch is initial.
17             message i802(g5).
18           else.
19             message e802(g5) into mv_message_text.
20             add_sy_message_to_results( changing cs_results = ms_results_global ).
21           endif.
22           return.
23         endif.
24
25         if ms_progpars-testrun       = abap_false and
26            ms_progpars-analysis_only = abap_false.
27           lv_activity = '16'.
28         else.
29           lv_activity = '48'.
30         endif.
31
32         "Check if the user has the required authorities
33         loop at ms_progpars-t_bukrs into data(ls_bukrs).
34
35           data(lv_tabix) = sy-tabix.
36
37           call function 'JV_AUTHORITY_CHECK_PROCESS'
38             exporting
39               bukrs        = ls_bukrs-bukrs
40               process_code = lv_process_code
41               activity     = lv_activity
42             exceptions
43               no_authority = 1.
44
45           if sy-subrc <> 0.
46             lv_auth_miss_count += 1.
47             delete ms_progpars-t_bukrs index lv_tabix.
48           endif.
49
50         endloop.
51
52         if lv_auth_miss_count > 0.
53           if lv_auth_miss_count = lv_jv_cocodes_count.
54             rv_error = abap_true.
55             if sy-batch is initial.
56               message i292(g4) with ls_bukrs-bukrs lv_process_code lv_activity.
57             else.
58               message e292(g4) with ls_bukrs-bukrs lv_process_code lv_activity into mv_message_text.
59               add_sy_message_to_results( changing cs_results = ms_results_global ).
60             endif.
61           endif.
62         endif.
63
64     *   Get the GL ledger
65         loop at ms_progpars-t_bukrs into ls_bukrs.
66           call function 'JV_GET_LEDGER'
67             exporting
68               iv_bukrs       = ls_bukrs-bukrs
69             importing
70               ev_rldnr       = ms_progpars-jv_ledger
71             exceptions
72               not_found      = 1
73               more_than_one  = 2
74               jva_not_active = 3
75               others         = 4.
76           if sy-subrc = 0 and
77              ms_progpars-jv_ledger is not initial.
78             exit.
79           endif.
80         endloop.
81
82       endmethod.

1        method add_item_info_for_doc.
2
3          data: ls_message      type bapiret2,
4                lv_message_text type string,
5                lv_docnr_string type string,
6                lt_log_doc      type gty_t_log.
7
8          lv_message_text = iv_msg_text.
9
10         if ms_progpars-do_not_store_issue_details = abap_false and
11            sy-batch is initial.
12           if is_critical_error( iv_msg_type ).
13             lv_message_text &&= ` - ` && 'Double click here for the details!'(dch).
14           endif.
15         endif.
16
17         if lines( it_doc_comb ) > 1.
18           loop at it_doc_comb assigning field-symbol(<ls_doc>).
19             if lv_docnr_string is not initial.
20               lv_docnr_string &&= `, `.
21             endif.
22             lv_docnr_string &&= condense( |{ <ls_doc>-docnr alpha = out }| ).
23           endloop.
24         endif.
25
26         loop at it_doc_comb into data(ls_doc).
27
28           clear lt_log_doc.
29
30           read table it_log transporting no fields
31                             with key rbukrs = ls_doc-rbukrs
32                                      ryear  = ls_doc-ryear
33                                      docnr  = ls_doc-docnr.
34           if sy-subrc = 0.
35             loop at it_log into data(ls_log)
36                            from sy-tabix.
37               if ls_log-rbukrs <> ls_doc-rbukrs or
38                  ls_log-ryear  <> ls_doc-ryear  or
39                  ls_log-docnr  <> ls_doc-docnr.
40                 exit.
41               endif.
42               append ls_log to lt_log_doc.
43             endloop.
44           else.
45             continue.
46           endif.
47
48           loop at lt_log_doc into data(ls_log_doc)
49                              where activ <> gc_jvlinetype-balancing_line.
50             exit.
51           endloop.
52
53           clear: ls_log_doc-docln,
54                  ls_log_doc-docln_acd,
55                  ls_log_doc-comment,
56                  ls_log_doc-messages.

63               exporting
64                 iv_msg_text     = 'Combined analysis for JV docs:'(cba) && ` ` && lv_docnr_string
65                 iv_msg_type     = gc_error_type-info
66               changing
67                 cs_log          = ls_log_doc ).
68           endif.
69
70           if is_doc-gl_no_of_lines = 0.
71             if lines( it_acdoca_doc[] ) = 0 and

75                   iv_msg_text     = conv string( 'ACDOCA document not found'(adn) )
76                   iv_msg_type     = gc_error_type-info
77                 changing
78                   cs_log          = ls_log_doc ).
79             endif.

84                   iv_msg_text     = 'JV and ACDOCA document have different number of lines'(dnl) && | ( { is_doc-jv_no_of_lines } <> { is_doc-gl_no_of_lines } )|
85                   iv_msg_type     = gc_error_type-info
86                 changing
87                   cs_log          = ls_log_doc ).
88             endif.
89           endif.

94             endif.
95           endif.

98                              where error is not initial

100            if iv_update = abap_true.
101              update_line_statistics_updates( ls_log ).
102            endif.

107                                        where type is not initial

110                endloop.
111              endif.
112            else.
113              "Add issue messages to main doc item
114              loop at ls_log-messages into ls_message
115                                      where type is not initial
116                                         or id   is not initial
117                                         or message(1) = '-'.   "Infos about value diffs
118                append ls_message to ls_log_doc-messages.
119              endloop.
120            endif.
121          endloop.
122

124          loop at is_doc-balances_log-messages into ls_message.

131                iv_updated  = iv_updated
132                iv_belnr    = iv_belnr
133                iv_gjahr    = iv_gjahr

136                io_messages = io_messages
137              changing
138                cs_log      = ls_log_doc ).
139          elseif iv_msg_text is not initial.

145            append ls_message to ls_log_doc-messages.

153            endif.
154          endif.

159                                               iv_new_error = iv_msg_type ).

1        method add_item_info_for_line.
2
3          data: ls_color     type lvc_s_scol,

35         endif.
36
37         if ms_progpars-show_all_lines = abap_true.
38           append ls_item_info to ms_results-t_item_info.
39         elseif ms_progpars-show_updates_and_errors = abap_true.
40           if iv_update = abap_true               or
41              is_log-balances_correct = abap_true or
42              ls_col-col <> 0.
43             append ls_item_info to ms_results-t_item_info.
44           endif.
45         elseif ms_progpars-show_errors_only = abap_true.
46           if ls_col-col = col_negative.
47             append ls_item_info to ms_results-t_item_info.
48           endif.
49         endif.
50
51       endmethod.

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
1        method calculate_task_packages.
2
3          data: lv_package_size_minimum type i value 10000,
4                lt_docs_per_period      type gty_t_docs_per_period,
5                ls_package              like line of rt_packages,
6                ls_periods              like line of ls_package-t_periods,
7                lv_cond                 type char255,
8                lv_new_total            type i.
9
10         count_jv_documents_by_period(
11           exporting
12             is_progpars        = is_progpars
13           changing
14             ct_docs_per_period = lt_docs_per_period ).
15
16         if lt_docs_per_period[] is initial.
17           return.
18         endif.
19
20         sort lt_docs_per_period by rbukrs ryear poper.
21
22         loop at lt_docs_per_period into data(ls_docs_per_period).
23
24           if ls_package-doc_count > 0.
25             lv_new_total = ls_package-doc_count + ls_docs_per_period-doc_count.
26             if ls_package-doc_count > ms_progpars-number_of_docs_per_task or
27                ( ls_package-doc_count >= lv_package_size_minimum and
28                  lv_new_total > ms_progpars-number_of_docs_per_task ).
29               append ls_package to rt_packages.
30               clear ls_package.
31             endif.
32           endif.
33
34           if ls_docs_per_period-doc_count > 0.
35             ls_package-doc_count += ls_docs_per_period-doc_count.
36             read table ls_package-t_periods assigning field-symbol(<ls_periods>)
37                                             with key ryear = ls_docs_per_period-ryear
38                                             binary search.
39             data(lv_tabix_periods) = sy-tabix.
40             if sy-subrc = 0 and <ls_periods> is assigned.
41               append ls_docs_per_period-poper to <ls_periods>-t_poper.
42             else.
43               ls_periods-ryear = ls_docs_per_period-ryear.
44               ls_periods-t_poper = value #( ( ls_docs_per_period-poper ) ).
45               insert ls_periods into ls_package-t_periods index lv_tabix_periods.
46             endif.
47           endif.
48
49         endloop.
50
51         if ls_package-doc_count > 0.
52           append ls_package to rt_packages.
53         endif.
54
55       endmethod.
*--- End of CL_JVA_MIG_CORRECT_ACDOCA=====CM02F - 55 lines ---

1        method process_data_parallelly.
2
3          data: lv_subrc         type sy-subrc,
4                lv_tabix_results type sy-tabix,
5                ls_taskdata      type gty_s_taskdata,
6                lv_package_no    type i,
7                ls_results_new   like line of mt_results,
8                ls_task_info     like line of ls_results_new-t_tasks.
9
10         clear mt_taskdata.
11
12         data(ls_progpars) = ms_progpars.
13         clear ls_progpars-t_bukrs.
14
15         loop at ms_progpars-t_bukrs into data(lv_bukrs).
16
17           ls_progpars = ms_progpars.
18           ls_progpars-bukrs = lv_bukrs.
19
20           "Add one item per company code to the results table
21           read table mt_results assigning field-symbol(<ls_results>)
22                                 with key bukrs = lv_bukrs
23                                 binary search.
24           if sy-subrc <> 0 or <ls_results> is not assigned.
25             ls_results_new-bukrs = ls_progpars-bukrs.
26             ls_results_new-s_progpars = ls_progpars.
27             ls_results_new-s_progpars-is_parallel_task = abap_true.
28             insert ls_results_new into mt_results index sy-tabix
29                                   assigning <ls_results>.
30           endif.
31
32           data(lt_packages) = calculate_task_packages( ls_progpars ).
33
34           if lines( lt_packages ) = 0.
35             message i708(gi) into mv_message_text.
36             add_sy_message_to_results( changing cs_results = <ls_results> ).
37             continue.
38           endif.
39
40           loop at lt_packages into data(ls_package).
41             if ls_package-doc_count > 0.
42               lv_package_no += 1.
43               clear ls_taskdata.
44               ls_taskdata-obj_id = ls_progpars-bukrs && `_` && lv_package_no.
45               ls_progpars-is_parallel_task = abap_true.
46               ls_progpars-s_task_package = ls_package.
47               ls_taskdata-s_progpars = ls_progpars.
48               append ls_taskdata to mt_taskdata.
49
50               ls_task_info-obj_id = ls_taskdata-obj_id.
51               ls_task_info-bukrs = ls_progpars-bukrs.
52               ls_task_info-s_progpars = ls_progpars.
53               append ls_task_info to <ls_results>-t_tasks.
54             endif.
55           endloop.
56
57           sort <ls_results>-t_tasks by obj_id.
58
59         endloop.
60
61         sort mt_taskdata by obj_id.
62
63         if lines( mt_taskdata ) <= 1.
64           "If there is only one task, there is no point in parallel processing
65           ms_progpars-parallel_processing = abap_false.
66           ms_results_global-s_progpars-parallel_processing = abap_false.
67           if sy-batch is initial and
68              ms_progpars-create_spools_in_foreground = abap_false.
69             ms_progpars-print_results = abap_false.
70           endif.
71           clear: mt_results,
72                  ms_results_global-t_tasks.
73           process_data_synchronously( ).
74           return.
75         endif.
76
77         "Execute the parallel processing
78         mo_output->spta_para_process_start_2( exporting
79                                                 iv_server_group             = ms_progpars-server_group
80                                                 iv_max_no_of_tasks          = ms_progpars-maximum_number_of_tasks
81                                                 iv_before_rfc_callback_form = 'BEFORE_RFC'
82                                                 iv_in_rfc_callback_form     = 'IN_RFC'
83                                                 iv_after_rfc_callback_form  = 'AFTER_RFC'
84                                                 iv_callback_prog            = ms_progpars-repid_callback
85                                               importing
86                                                 ev_subrc                    = lv_subrc ).
87
88         if lv_subrc > 0.
89
90           "Show parallelization error message
91           case lv_subrc.
92             when 1.
93               message i719(shdb_pfw) with ms_progpars-server_group into mv_message_text.
94             when 2.
95               message i005(spta) with ms_progpars-server_group into mv_message_text.
96             when 3.
97               message i160(fcmlacc) into mv_message_text.
98           endcase.
99
100          add_sy_message_to_results( changing cs_results = ms_results_global ).
101
102        endif.
103
104      endmethod.

1        method count_jv_documents_by_period.
2
3          data: ct_docs_per_period_op like ct_docs_per_period.
4
5          if is_progpars-select_fi_documents = abap_true or
6             is_progpars-select_co_documents = abap_true.
7
8            data(lt_ref_cond) = make_reference_condition( is_progpars ).
9
10           select rbukrs, ryear, poper, count( distinct docnr ) as doc_count
11                  from jvso1
12                  where rldnr     = '4A'
13                    and rbukrs    = @is_progpars-bukrs
14                    and ryear    in @is_progpars-r_ryear
15                    and poper    in @is_progpars-r_poper
16                    and (lt_ref_cond)
17                    and budat    in @is_progpars-r_budat
18                    and bldat    in @is_progpars-r_bldat
19                    and racct    in @is_progpars-r_racct
20                    and rjvnam   in @is_progpars-r_rjvnam
21                    and regrou   in @is_progpars-r_regrou
22                    and rrecin   in @is_progpars-r_rrecin
23                    and bilid    in @is_progpars-r_bilid
24                    and prodper  in @is_progpars-r_prodper
25                    and rcntr    in @is_progpars-r_rcntr
26                    and rordnr   in @is_progpars-r_rordnr
27                    and rprojk   in @is_progpars-r_rprojk
28                    and nplnr    in @is_progpars-r_nplnr
29                    and aufpl    in @is_progpars-r_aufpl
30                    and aplzl    in @is_progpars-r_aplzl
31                  group by rbukrs, ryear, poper
32                  order by rbukrs, ryear, poper
33                  into corresponding fields of table @ct_docs_per_period. "#EC CI_NOFIELD
34
35         else.
36
37           if is_progpars-r_activ_me[] is not initial.
38             select rbukrs, ryear, poper, count( distinct docnr ) as doc_count
39                    from jvso1
40                    where rldnr  = '4A'
41                      and rbukrs = @is_progpars-bukrs
42                      and ryear in @is_progpars-r_ryear
43                      and poper in @is_progpars-r_poper
44                      and docnr in @is_progpars-r_docnr
45                      and activ in @is_progpars-r_activ_me
46                    group by rbukrs, ryear, poper
47                    order by rbukrs, ryear, poper
48                    into corresponding fields of table @ct_docs_per_period. "#EC CI_NOFIELD
49           endif.
50
51           if is_progpars-r_activ_op[] is not initial.
52             select rbukrs, ryear, poper, count( distinct docnr ) as doc_count
53                    from jvso2
54                    where rldnr   = '4B'
55                      and rbukrs  = @is_progpars-bukrs
56                      and ryear  in @is_progpars-r_ryear
57                      and poper  in @is_progpars-r_poper
58                      and docnr  in @is_progpars-r_docnr
59                      and activ  in @is_progpars-r_activ_op
60                    group by rbukrs, ryear, poper
61                    order by rbukrs, ryear, poper
62                    into corresponding fields of table @ct_docs_per_period_op. "#EC CI_NOFIELD
63
64             if ct_docs_per_period_op[] is not initial.
65               if ct_docs_per_period[] is initial.
66                 ct_docs_per_period[] = ct_docs_per_period_op[].
67               else.
68                 loop at ct_docs_per_period_op assigning field-symbol(<ls_docs_per_period_op>).
69                   read table ct_docs_per_period assigning field-symbol(<ls_docs_per_period>)
70                                                 with key rbukrs = <ls_docs_per_period_op>-rbukrs
71                                                          ryear  = <ls_docs_per_period_op>-ryear
72                                                          poper  = <ls_docs_per_period_op>-poper
73                                                 binary search.
74                   data(lv_tabix) = sy-tabix.
75                   if sy-subrc = 0 and <ls_docs_per_period> is assigned.
76                     <ls_docs_per_period>-doc_count += <ls_docs_per_period_op>-doc_count.
77                   else.
78                     insert <ls_docs_per_period_op> into ct_docs_per_period index lv_tabix.
79                   endif.
80                 endloop.
81               endif.
82             endif.
83           endif.
84
85         endif.
86
87       endmethod.                    "count_jv_documents_by_period

48                    where rldnr   = '4B'
49                      and rbukrs in @is_progpars-r_bukrs
50                      and ryear  in @is_progpars-r_ryear
51                      and poper  in @is_progpars-r_poper
52                      and docnr  in @is_progpars-r_docnr
53                      and activ  in @is_progpars-r_activ_op
54                    into @data(lv_count_op).                 "#EC CI_NOFIELD
55
56             rv_count += lv_count_op.
57           endif.
58
59         endif.

1        method update_task_info.
2
3          read table cs_results-t_tasks
4                     assigning field-symbol(<fs_tasks>)
5                     with key obj_id = is_taskdata-obj_id
6                     binary search.
7
8          if <fs_tasks> is assigned.
9            <fs_tasks>-returned = abap_true.
10           <fs_tasks>-scheduled = is_taskdata-scheduled.
11           <fs_tasks>-initialized = is_taskdata-initialized.
12           <fs_tasks>-processed = is_taskdata-processed.
13           <fs_tasks>-runtime = is_taskdata-runtime.
14         endif.
15
16         cs_results-tasks_returned += 1.
17         cs_results-tasks_executed += 1.
18         if is_taskdata-scheduled = abap_true.
19           cs_results-tasks_scheduled += 1.
20         endif.
21         if is_taskdata-initialized = abap_true.
22           cs_results-tasks_initialized += 1.
23         endif.
24         if is_taskdata-processed = abap_true.
25           cs_results-tasks_processed += 1.
26         endif.
27
28         cs_results-runtime_tasks += is_taskdata-runtime.
29         cs_results-runtime_tasks_admin += is_taskdata-s_results-runtime_tasks_admin.
30
31         cs_results-runtime_cocode += is_taskdata-runtime + is_taskdata-s_results-runtime_tasks_admin.
32
33       endmethod.

1        method save_results_global.
2
3          if lines( mt_results[] ) > 0.
4
5            loop at mt_results assigning field-symbol(<ls_results>).
6
7              add_up_results_stats_totals( changing cs_results = <ls_results> ).
8
9              append lines of <ls_results>-t_tasks to ms_results_global-t_tasks.
10             ms_results_global-runtime_tasks       += <ls_results>-runtime_tasks.
11             ms_results_global-runtime_tasks_admin += <ls_results>-runtime_tasks_admin.
12
13             ms_results_global-tasks_scheduled   += <ls_results>-tasks_scheduled.
14             ms_results_global-tasks_initialized += <ls_results>-tasks_initialized.
15             ms_results_global-tasks_executed    += <ls_results>-tasks_executed.
16             ms_results_global-tasks_processed   += <ls_results>-tasks_processed.
17             ms_results_global-tasks_returned    += <ls_results>-tasks_returned.
18
19             append lines of <ls_results>-t_spool to ms_results_global-t_spool.
20
21             add_up_all_results_stats(
22               exporting
23                 is_results_to_add = <ls_results>
24               changing
25                 cs_results        = ms_results_global ).
26
27             if ms_progpars-print_results = abap_true and
28                <ls_results>-t_tasks[] is not initial and
29                <ls_results>-t_spool[] is initial.
30               <ls_results>-t_spool = cl_jva_mig_alv_output=>get_instance( )->print_results( is_results = <ls_results> ).
31               clear <ls_results>-t_item_info.
32             endif.
33
34           endloop.
35
36         else.
37
38           add_up_all_results_stats(
39             exporting
40               is_results_to_add = ms_results
41             changing
42               cs_results        = ms_results_global ).
43
44         endif.
45
46         add_up_results_stats_totals( changing cs_results = ms_results_global ).
47
48         calculate_global_runtimes( ).
49
50         if ms_progpars-print_results = abap_true.
51           data(lt_spool) = cl_jva_mig_alv_output=>get_instance( )->print_results(
52                              it_results = mt_results
53                              is_results = ms_results_global ).
54           append lines of ms_results_global-t_spool to lt_spool.
55           ms_results_global-t_spool[] = lt_spool[].
56         endif.
57
58       endmethod.

1        method get_ledgers.
2
3          data: lt_ledgers type rldnr_t.
4
5
6          "Temporary: Update the new field RLDNR_ACD in the log table
7          update jva_acd_upd_log set rldnr_acd = @ms_progpars-jv_ledger
8                                 where rbukrs = @iv_bukrs
9                                   and rldnr_acd is initial.
10         commit work.
11
12
13         refresh: mt_ledgers, mr_ledgers.
14
15         if ms_progpars-correct_all_ledgers <> abap_true.
16           mt_ledgers = value #( ( rldnr = ms_progpars-jv_ledger ) ).
17           mr_ledgers = value #( ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = ms_progpars-jv_ledger ) ).
18           return.
19         endif.
20
21         select rldnr from finsc_ld_cmp
22                      where bukrs = @iv_bukrs
23                      order by rldnr
24                      into table @lt_ledgers
25                      bypassing buffer.
26
27         mt_ledgers = value #( ( rldnr = ms_progpars-jv_ledger ) ).
28         loop at lt_ledgers into data(lv_ledger).
29           if lv_ledger <> ms_progpars-jv_ledger.
30             mt_ledgers = value #( base mt_ledgers
31                                   ( rldnr = lv_ledger ) ).
32           endif.
33           mr_ledgers = value #( base mr_ledgers
34                                 ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = lv_ledger ) ).
35         endloop.
36
37       endmethod.

821        mo_cut->ms_progpars-update_existing_lines = abap_false.
822        mo_cut->ms_progpars-update_co_in_co_lines = abap_true.
823        mo_cut->ms_progpars-update_co_in_bs_lines = abap_true.
824
825        lv_corr_type = mo_cut->determine_correction_type(
826            iv_corr_type  = space
827            iv_issue_type = mo_cut->gc_issue_type-wrong_co_data
828            iv_is_co_line = abap_true ).

831            act  = lv_corr_type
832            exp  = mo_cut->gc_corr_type-update_line
833            quit = if_abap_unit_constant=>quit-no ).
834

836            iv_corr_type  = space
837            iv_issue_type = mo_cut->gc_issue_type-wrong_co_data
838            iv_is_co_line = abap_false ).
839
840        cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
841            act  = lv_corr_type

843            quit = if_abap_unit_constant=>quit-no ).
844
845
846    *      when gc_issue_type-wrong_cost_center or
847    *           gc_issue_type-wrong_order       or
848    *           gc_issue_type-wrong_wbs_element or
849    *           gc_issue_type-wrong_network     or
850    *           gc_issue_type-wrong_co_data.
851    *
852    *        if iv_is_co_line = abap_true.
853    *          if ms_progpars-repost_co_in_co_lines = abap_true.
854    *            lv_corr_type = gc_corr_type-rev_and_repost_line.
855    *          elseif ms_progpars-update_co_in_co_lines = abap_true or
856    *                 ms_progpars-update_existing_lines = abap_true.
857    *            lv_corr_type = gc_corr_type-update_line.
858    *          endif.
859    *        else.
860    *          if ms_progpars-repost_co_in_bs_lines = abap_true.
861    *            lv_corr_type = gc_corr_type-rev_and_repost_line.
862    *          elseif ms_progpars-update_co_in_bs_lines = abap_true or
863    *                 ms_progpars-update_existing_lines = abap_true.
864    *            lv_corr_type = gc_corr_type-update_line.
865    *          endif.
866    *        endif.
867    *
868    *    endcase.
869
870      endmethod.

1936     method add_compression_message.
1937
1938       constants: lc_belnr type belnr_d value '0000000001',
1939                  lc_buzei type buzei   value '001'.
1940
1941       data: ls_jvso          type jvso,
1942             ls_log           type cl_jva_mig_correct_acdoca=>gty_s_log,
1943             lt_jv_compr_info type  cl_jva_mig_correct_acdoca=>gty_t_jv_compr_info.
1944
1945       ls_jvso = value #( reffidoc = lc_belnr
1946                          reffidln = lc_buzei ).
1947
1948       mo_cut->add_compression_message(
1949         exporting
1950           is_jvso          = ls_jvso
1951         changing
1952           cs_log           = ls_log
